## 0. The state this unit exists to clear — and the two rulings it carries

**Gate 1 on the first, wider fixture proposal returned `role_validity` = `FLAWED` and `role_critique` =
`NEEDS-REWORK`.** `docs/next-steps.md`'s `CURRENT WORK` head records both verdicts with their findings (the
tracker's own arithmetic: a `FLAWED` validity set plus a `NEEDS-REWORK` critique set, **11 + 10 findings**,
`RECORDED READING; measurer: the gate-1 reviewers`), and records that **the architecture and change-analysis steps
of that gate were NOT spent** — *a proposal whose validity is `FLAWED` must be re-shaped before they can review it*.
**This filing is the re-shaping of the DECLARATION half only.**

### 0.1 The five findings that decide this unit's shape (each quoted from its recorder, not re-derived)

| # | The reading, in substance | Its recorder |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **G-1** | **THE DRIVER IS ALREADY AHEAD OF THE FILED PROPOSAL: its corpus-REPORTING half LANDED and is PINNED.** `ufBlockPrecondition()`, the 17-key `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS`, the `PRECONDITION-FAILED`/`fixture-missing` markers and `parkReason` are live (`scripts/live-drive.mjs`) and hold in the pin (`tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts`), so the red set must name **the one sentence of `GAP-8` still unmet**, never *"the driver lacks a fixture"*. | `docs/next-steps.md`, the gate-1 anchored insert (`RECORDED READING; measurer: the gate-1 reviewers`); the four named symbols are `VERIFIED-BY-READ` by this filing |
| **G-2** | **THE ROWS `GAP-8` / `LIVE-FIXTURE-PRECONDITION-GAPS` ACTUALLY NAMES ARE NOT IN THE LANDED SET.** The row's fixture group is the **ENGINE** (`ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:8080`, the `gnosis_*` rows) and the **226-DOCUMENT O-0 CORPUS** (`--strict-seed` / `--o0-corpus=226`; *claimed 226 · observed 2*) — and the filed proposal parked the engine half and mocked neither. | `docs/defects.md` `LIVE-FIXTURE-PRECONDITION-GAPS`; `docs/next-steps.md`'s `CURRENT WORK` item `2` (`RECORDED READING; measurer: the live-scenario runner` + the operator) |
| **G-3** | **THE HONEST TOOTH IS A CHECKABLE DECLARATION — A DERIVATION THAT FAILS WHEN A CORPUS-READING BLOCK IS ABSENT FROM THE DECLARATION — not an advisory prose claim.** The landed set is an **unchecked hand-list**: nothing in the tree holds it to what the driver's blocks actually read. | this filing's own read (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`): the pin's corpus-facing arms read the *shape* of the precondition machinery (§0.3 `V-4`), never the set's completeness |

### 0.2 The architect's ruled SHAPE (the two `docs/decisions.md` rows, and the split)

1. **THE FIXTURE'S FORM (`DECIDED: MOCK-DATA-SETS-AND-A-LAUNCH-SELECTION-ARG`, clause (1))**: **hand-authored MOCK
   DATA SETS**, plus **an arg selecting which set the live demo launches with** — *not* a corpus synthesized from an
   args-derived shape rule, and *not* a seeded import pipeline.
2. **IT MUST BE DECLARED IN THE RUN (same row, clause (2))**: the selection is part of the run's identity; the
   artifact must name **which data set** the run used, *"so a reading can be attributed to its fixture without
   reading the operator's command line"*.
3. **THE PRE-EXISTING SET IS OBSOLETE (same row, clause (3))**: the `.live-corpus/*` seed route (`seedCorpus()`),
   `o0MarkdownTree(seedDir)` / `--seed=` / `--corpus-root=` / `--strict-seed`, and the 226-document O-0 corpus route
   (`--o0-corpus=226`) are **OBSOLETE** (the data engine is being rebuilt) and **must not be extended or used as the
   fixture's foundation**. **The row's own scope sentence is carried verbatim in substance: the O-0 measurement /
   report MECHANISM is a different object and is NOT declared obsolete** — which is why this unit reads the `o0_*`
   blocks' corpus surface without condemning the mechanism (`§2.2` census rows `45`–`49`'s note, `§3` `R-3`).
4. **THE HONESTY CLAUSE (`DECIDED: GAP-8-INTERIM-MOCK-CORPUS-FROM-CALLING-ARGS`, clause (2))**: **a mock is a
   DECLARED INPUT FIXTURE, NEVER AN ORACLE** — it may never turn a proxy probe into a PASS (`D-GP-UFA-2` stands
   untouched), **every row it feeds must still assert a real user-visible end state on the ASSEMBLED surface**, and
   **the run's artifact must say what the fixture was** (`D-GP-UFA-3`'s falsifiability + real-input proof unchanged).
   **A mocked/no-data-fixture reading may never be quoted as a live-corpus reading.**
5. **THE WORK IS SPLIT, AND THIS FILING IS UNIT A**:
   - **UNIT A (this spec) = the fixture DECLARATION + the park honesty in the driver.**
   - **UNIT B (later, a separate unit) = the mock DATA SETS themselves + the selection arg + the obsolete set's
     disposition.**
   **Consequence, stated as a denial**: **Unit A authors NO data set, adds NO selection arg, and touches the obsolete
   seed route only to NAME it obsolete** (`§3`, `§9`).

### 0.4 THE HONESTY BLOCK

1. **This filing ran nothing.** No battery, no leg, no Electron, no suite, no trio. **Its tool wall is read/search +
   doc-write.**
2. **It authors no data set, no arg, no test and no driver edit.** `§2`'s census is a **read of the driver source**,
   and the declaration it hands forward is **a contract, not a landing**.
3. **`G-1`…`G-5` are quoted with their recorders**; `V-1`…`V-10` are reads this filing took and are labelled as such.
   **No figure here is re-derived from another pass's number.**
4. **`NOT RECORDED`, each with what settles it:** the gate-1 reviews' finding-id lists (the tracker records the
   counts, `11 + 10`, and their substance in prose — **what settles them: the gate-1 record file, if the supervisor
   holds it**); the exact `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS` membership **as a set** beyond the `17` key names read here
   (**settled: `VERIFIED-BY-READ` in `§2.1`**); and the exit code of any fixture-absent run (**settled by `§8`'s
   class (b) battery**).
5. **The `sha256` and line count of THIS FILE are OWED** at `§11` item 4 — this pass holds no shell.
   **⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`: `§11` item 4 IS `§12` item 4 (this filing's own numbering: the owed-items
   table is `§12`), and the `sha256` REMAINS `OWED` AT ITS STATED SITES (`§12` item 4, `§15.8`, `§16.0`); the
   LINE COUNT is a `VERIFIED-BY-READ` figure of the file-read tool's own line census, restated at `§17.1`.
   The `§11`-for-`§12` slip of this item's own sentence is SUPERSEDED and kept visible rather than silently
   renumbered.⟩**

## 1. Scope

### 1.1 What UNIT A IS (the deliverable, in one list)

1. **THE CENSUS, ESTABLISHED BY READ AND NAMED** — every `BLOCKS` key that reads a corpus-derived surface, listed by
   name with the surface and the read site (`§2`). **The declaration is only as good as this census.**
2. **THE DECLARATION, AND ITS CHECKABILITY** — every censused block declares a fixture dependency; the declaration
   is held to the census **by DERIVATION from the driver source**, with one arm failing when a corpus-reading block
   is absent from the declaration and a second failing when a declared block has no corpus-reading path (`§4`).
3. **THE PARK DISCIPLINE AND `RCA-11` CLAUSE (b)** — a block whose declared fixture is absent parks **by name**,
   carrying its **DECLARED row id(s)** and a **`parkReason` naming the fixture**; **never an app FAIL** and **never a
   silent `rows=0` read**; and **every censused block's park reason is classified fixture-missing vs structurally
   non-exercisable, with what settles it** (`§5`).
4. **THE RUN-WIDE FIXTURE STATE, PRINTED** — a field/line on **every** run (not only O-0 runs) stating the fixture
   state the run used, which may never be omitted; under Unit A (Unit B not landed) that state is
   **`no fixture data set selected`** (`§6`).
5. **THE HONESTY CLAUSE, CARRIED** — a fixture is an INPUT, never an oracle; no proxy PASS; every row still asserts
   a real user-visible end state on the ASSEMBLED surface; a mocked/no-data run must be readable as such from its
   own artifact (`§7`).

### 1.2 ALLOWED surface (exact)

| Path | The grant |
| --- | --- |
| **`scripts/live-drive.mjs`** | **THE DELIVERABLE.** The fixture DECLARATION constant and its census membership, the block-runner's use of the declaration in place of the hand-list, the `parkReason` text for the fixture-absent route, and the run-wide fixture-state field/line. **No data set, no arg, no seed-route change.** |
| **`tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts`** | **NAMED as the unit's structural pin.** Its rules are **kept green or RE-STATED by the TestWriter under the program's re-statement discipline** (`⟨RE-STATED …⟩`, superseded value visible, teeth kept, **never a relaxation**). **The TestWriter authors the changes to this file** (`AGENTS.md` item 3). |
| **`docs/specs/unit-live-fixture-precondition-declaration.md`** (this file) | The contract. **Amended only by a spec-owning pass, annotated beside** (`RCA-8(c)`). |
| **`docs/next-steps.md`** | **NOT this unit's write** — the landing pass's DONE row and the supervisor's tracker edits are theirs; this filing records what they must state (`§11`). |

### 1.3 DENIED surface (explicit, so the allow-list cannot be widened by implication)

- **`src/**`** — no app change of any kind; this unit repairs no app-layer reading.
- **The MCP contract** (`docs/specs/mcp-endpoint.md`) and any MCP tool/argument — **no new tool, no new argument, no
  change to a reply shape**.
- **The engine** — no engine work, no engine fixture, no `gnosis.*` change. **The `gnosis_*` rows' absent-engine
  precondition is NOT this unit's** (`G-2`; `DECIDED: GNOSIS-ENGINE-READY-AND-SHELL-INTEGRATION-BEHIND-THE-UI-OVERHAUL`).
- **`MATRIX_ROWS`' row set — FULL AT `8`** (`U-1`..`U-8`): no id added, removed, renumbered or re-mapped. **A new live
  assertion enters as a RE-PIN, or as an extended row in `ROW_EXTENDED` — never as a `§5.U` slot.**
- **A new `BLOCKS` key** — none. The driver's key census is **frozen at `100`** by the pin's `BLOCK_CENSUS_SET` /
  `BLOCK_CENSUS_DIGEST`; a new key is a review finding unless the TestWriter re-states the frozen census **with both
  values visible**.
- **The obsolete seed / O-0-corpus route AS A FOUNDATION** — `seedCorpus()`, `o0MarkdownTree()`, `--seed=`,
  `--corpus-root=`, `--strict-seed`, `--o0-corpus=` are **NAMED obsolete and left exactly as they are**
  (`DECIDED: MOCK-DATA-SETS-AND-A-LAUNCH-SELECTION-ARG` clause (3)). **Unit A neither repairs nor extends them.**
- **Any `--block=`-space-only claim** — a rule that holds only for the blocks an operator happens to request in a
  scoped run **is not a property of the driver**. The declaration, the derivation and the run-wide fixture state must
  hold for **`--block=all` and for every scoped invocation alike** (`§4.4`, `§8.3`).
- **`docs/skills/designing-pages.md`, its test-use-case coverage matrix and the demo-page index** — **untouched**:
  this unit changes no page design, so the design-skill update obligation **does not attach** (`§1.5`).
- **`package.json`, `vitest.config.ts`, `npm run` scripts, the divergence leg** — untouched.

### 1.4 What this unit is NOT

It is **not** UNIT B: it authors **no** mock data set, **no** selection arg, and **no** disposition of the obsolete
set beyond naming it obsolete. It is **not** an app repair, a re-measurement, or a claim that any row passes. It is
**not** a re-opening of the landed live-driver unit: it **EXTENDS** that unit's contract (`§10` `1`). It is **not**
the engine fixture and **not** the O-0 corpus fixture. **It adds no live assertion about the app** — every row it
touches keeps its existing assertion; what changes is **how the driver reports a fixture it does not have**.

### 1.5 The design-skill obligation, explicitly discharged

**No page design, no page layout, no component markup, no test-use-case coverage row and no demo-page index entry is
touched or required by this unit.** The obligation in `AGENTS.md`'s page-design rule attaches to a change that
affects page design; this unit's whole surface is the **instrument** (`scripts/live-drive.mjs`) and its **pin**.
**A landing pass that edits `docs/skills/designing-pages.md` for this unit is doing unauthorised work; a landing pass
that ignores it is compliant.**

## 2. THE CENSUS — every `BLOCKS` key that reads a corpus-derived surface

### 2.1 The read procedure (stated so the census is reproducible, not asserted)

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 2 AND 3 — THE FOUR TOKEN PASSES AS FILED ARE SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE BELOW. THE PROCEDURE OF RECORD IS THE PREDICATE + READ-GRAMMAR + MASKING CONVENTION + HELPER-CLOSURE RULE STATED HERE.⟩**

**2.1.1 THE PREDICATE (what makes a read corpus-DEPENDENT).** **A READ IS *CORPUS-DEPENDENT* iff it is either**

1. **an MCP read of the document STORE** — `rag.list_documents` · `rag.get_document` · `rag.query` · `edit.set_content`, **or** an MCP call whose ARGUMENT names a corpus document id or node id (`provident.focus` with a `document`/`nodeId`/`documentId` target, `rag.query` with a `filters.target.documentId`/`documentPathPrefix`); **or**
2. **a DOM read whose SELECTOR or read ATTRIBUTE is keyed by a corpus identity or a corpus-derived value** — `[data-document-id]` · `[data-folder-path]` · `[data-folder-label]` · `[data-rag-node-id]` · `[data-edit-surface]` / `#page-edit-surface` / `UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` · a tab or row matched by a corpus document's NAME (`/alpha/`, `.tab` + `kind === 'document'`) · `[contenteditable]` read **inside the stage** (`#zone:main`) · a read routed through a corpus-reading HELPER (`2.1.4`).

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` FINDINGS `F-1` + `F-2` — THE PREDICATE'S OPERATIONAL FORM. FOUR CLAUSES, EACH DECIDING ONE CONTESTED KEY OF `§2.2`'s TABLE, SO THE CENSUS COUNT FOLLOWS FROM A RULE AND FROM NOTHING ELSE. EVERY ONE IS `VERIFIED-BY-READ` AT THE SITE IT NAMES (`reader: this third amendment pass`).⟩**

- **`2.1.1-A` — THE STORE-CALL CLAUSE (it keeps `boot_landing` IN).** Limb 1 is satisfied by **an MCP STORE CALL IN CALL POSITION WHOSE ARGUMENT NAMES A DOCUMENT PATH OR ID** — the driver's own call forms are `h.mcpTool(h.mcp, '<tool>', args)`, `h.mcpRead('<tool>', args)` and `mcpToolResult(mcp, '<tool>', args)`, **including a tool name reached through a ONE-HOP LOCAL BINDING whose value is that short literal (`ufBlockPrecondition`'s own `const tool = 'rag.list_documents'`, `VERIFIED-BY-READ`)** — and it therefore covers the store **WRITE** calls **`edit.set_content`** and **`edit.import_markdown`** whose argument is a document path / `nodeId`. **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `boot_landing`'s own body: its ONLY corpus-adjacent act is `h.mcpRead('edit.import_markdown', { files: [doc] })` with `doc = join(ROOT, '.live-corpus', 'live4-first.md')` — a store call whose argument names a document path — so `boot_landing` IS a census member.** **THE `selfProvisioning` CARVE-OUT IS A CARVE-OUT FROM THE PARK GATE, NEVER FROM THE CENSUS**: a block that supplies its own input still reads the store content it placed, and `§4.1` records exactly that through `selfProvisioning:true` (`§5.2`'s `SELF/FM` rows already read it that way).
- **`2.1.1-B` — THE CALL-POSITION (PROSE) CLAUSE (it keeps `uf_search_2` OUT; this IS `F-2`'s DISCRIMINATOR).** **A store-method NAME counts only in CALL POSITION — the tool-name argument position of one of the driver's MCP call forms (`2.1.1-A`). A name that appears ONLY inside PROSE — a comment, or a template/string literal's text that is NOT in that argument position — is NOT a store read, whatever the masking convention leaves readable.** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `uf_search_2`'s own body: its only `rag.query` occurrence is inside a ~100-character RESULT-STRING sentence (`'The advanced-search disclosure exposes the full rag.query arg surface, painted, in both directions'`) — not a call, and not any call's argument — so `uf_search_2` is OUT.**
- **`2.1.1-C` — THE `M-NULL` CONSUMPTION CLAUSE (it keeps `x_flat` OUT; `F-2`'s second limb).** A limb-1 read requires **the call's RESULT to be CONSUMED AS STORE CONTENT — operationally: bound to a name that is later read in a DOCUMENT-IDENTITY access (`documents`, `documentId`, `nodes`, `edges`, and `map(…)`/`[0]`/`.length` over those) — OR the call's ARGUMENTS to name a document id/node id (`filters.target.documentId`, `filters.documentPathPrefix`, `{documentId}`, `{nodeId}`)**. **A store call whose result is only stringified into a diagnostic, audited, or streamed — and whose arguments name no document/node id — is class `M-null`: RECORDED in `§2.3` and NEVER a census member.** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `x_flat`'s own body: `h.mcpTool(h.mcp, 'rag.query', { query: 'alpha', topK: 3 })` — NO document/node-id argument — with its result read only as `q.results || q.result` (a retrieval-hit list, never a document identity). `M-null`, OUT.**
- **`2.1.1-D` — THE IDENTITY-USE CLAUSE FOR THE EDIT SURFACE (it keeps `stage_boot_landing_diag` and `stage_multimount_reachability` OUT).** **The limb-2 entry `[data-edit-surface]` / `#page-edit-surface` / `UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` means: a read that USES the edit surface's identity AS a CORPUS IDENTITY — i.e. compares the marker's VALUE to a document id, or uses it as a document key. A helper whose ONLY corpus-shaped token is that identity read AS AN OPAQUE presence/agreement signal carries no corpus-IDENTITY read and is NOT a closure seed (`2.1.4`).** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `ufSurfaceCensus`'s own body: it collects `byId`/`byMarker`, compares them BY ELEMENT IDENTITY (`byId.every((e, i) => e === byMarker[i])`), returns the marker values only as an `ids` array plus boxes, and never compares a marker to a document id — so it is NOT a seed, and both of its callers are OUT by `2.1.4`.** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` AT THE COUNTER-CASE, so the clause is a narrowing and not a blanket demotion: `stage_surface_census_i2r`'s OWN body DOES compare identity (`census.ids[0] === v.editSurface`) and reaches `ufEnsureDocumentSurface`, whose OWN body carries the literal `#pane-doc-nav [data-document-id]` — so it stays IN on its own limbs.** **⟨THE COUNTER-READING IS RECORDED, NOT SMOOTHED: a reader may read `ufSurfaceCensus`'s `ids` return as a corpus-identity read and therefore ADMIT both callers. THE RULING IS `2.1.1-D`; `A-1` must PRINT both keys with the clause applied if its own derivation admits them (`§4.2`), and `§15.8`'s figure of record is the one this clause yields.⟩**

**A read of a merely corpus-SHAPED selector is NOT corpus-dependent**: `#pane-doc-nav` as a FRAME, `#pane-search` as a PANE, a bare `li` count inside either, `.pane-frame[data-pane-id]`, `#operator-enabled-panes`, the persisted pane/tab STATE objects. **A frame exists with an empty corpus; a document row does not.** **THE `selfProvisioning` CARVE-OUT (one limb, stated because it is what keeps `boot_landing`/`import1` honest): a read of a file the block ITSELF writes in the same body, then imports (`edit.import_markdown`), is NOT a dependency on a PRE-EXISTING corpus** — the block supplies its own input, and the declaration says so through `selfProvisioning:true`.

**2.1.2 THE READ-GRAMMAR (the operational form of the predicate — the FILED TOKEN LIST, RE-DERIVED, NOT A SUBSTITUTE FOR A DERIVATION).** **`DERIVED-OUTPUT ONLY; NEVER THE PROCEDURE` (`§4.2` forbids the token form as the rule).** **⟨ADDED `2026-10-03`: THE THREE BULLETS IMMEDIATELY BELOW ARE THE SECOND AMENDMENT'S CLASSES, KEPT VISIBLE AS ITS READING; THE CLASSES OF RECORD ARE THE `2026-10-03` RE-STATEMENT AT THE END OF THIS LIST (`2.1.1-A`…`D`). WHERE THEY DIFFER, THE RE-STATEMENT GOVERNS.⟩** The predicates of `2.1.1` are applied as these token classes, **read in CODE position only** (`2.1.3`) and **closed transitively over the helper graph** (`2.1.4`):

- **class `M` — MCP store reads:** `rag.list_documents` · `rag.get_document` · `rag.query` · `edit.set_content` · `provident.get_journal` is **NOT** in this class (it reads the journal, not the store), and a bare `rag.query` whose RESULT is never read as documents (`x_flat`'s form) is **class `M-null`**: recorded, not counted as a census member (`§2.3` `P-8`);
- **class `S` — corpus-keyed DOM reads:** `data-document-id` · `data-folder-path` · `data-folder-label` · `data-rag-node-id` · `data-edit-surface` · `UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` · `#page-edit-surface` · `O0_DOCUMENT_SELECTOR` · `O0_FOLDER_SELECTOR` · `openDocumentTab` · a `/alpha/`- or `kind === 'document'`-filtered tab;
- **class `H` — corpus-reading HELPERS (the seeds of the closure):** `ufEnsureDocumentSurface` · `ufEnsureEditFixture` · `ufOpenDocumentById` · `ufPaneSearch` · `ufDocNavRow` · `o0FolderRows` · `o0ResetFolderState` · `o0Census` · `ufEnsurePaneExpanded` is **NOT** a seed (it resolves pane visibility, not corpus content).
**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` `F-1`/`F-2` — THE THREE CLASSES RE-STATED ON `2.1.1-A`…`D`, WITH THE ONE ADDED TOKEN, THE ONE NARROWED ENTRY AND THE TWO NON-SEEDS NAMED:⟩**
- **class `M` (amended by ONE ADDITION):** `rag.list_documents` · `rag.get_document` · `rag.query` · `edit.set_content` · **`edit.import_markdown` (`2.1.1-A`: the store WRITE call whose argument is a document path)** — **every member read IN CALL POSITION (`2.1.1-B`) and consumed as store content or carrying a document/node-id argument (`2.1.1-C`)**. `provident.get_journal` is **NOT** class `M` (it reads the JOURNAL, not the store). A bare `rag.query` whose result is never read as documents stays class **`M-null`** (`x_flat`'s form, `§2.3` `P-8`) and satisfies NEITHER limb. **A `rag.*`/`edit.*` name sitting inside PROSE is not a token of this class at all** (`uf_search_2`, `§2.3` `P-5`).
- **class `S` (amended by ONE NARROWING):** `data-document-id` · `data-folder-path` · `data-folder-label` · `data-rag-node-id` · `data-edit-surface` · `UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` · `#page-edit-surface` · `O0_DOCUMENT_SELECTOR` · `O0_FOLDER_SELECTOR` · `openDocumentTab` · a `/alpha/`- or `kind === 'document'`-filtered tab — **with the `data-edit-surface`/`#page-edit-surface`/`UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` entries READ THROUGH `2.1.1-D`: an identity USE, not an attribute presence.**
- **class `H` (amended by ONE SUBTRACTION, and the subtraction is a NAMING rather than a deletion — neither key was ever on this list):** the seeds of the closure remain `ufEnsureDocumentSurface` · `ufEnsureEditFixture` · `ufOpenDocumentById` · `ufPaneSearch` · `ufDocNavRow` · `o0FolderRows` · `o0ResetFolderState` · `o0Census`, and **`ufSurfaceCensus` and `ufEditSurfaceState` are named as NON-SEEDS** (both read the edit-surface marker as an opaque presence/agreement/state signal, `2.1.1-D`); a reader that seeds them is admitted ONLY under the recorded counter-reading of `2.1.1-D`, and must then print the two keys it admits.
- **class `MISSING-TOKEN`, DELETED FROM THE FILED LIST:** `#pane-doc-nav` and `#pane-search li` as bare selectors, `.live-corpus` as a bare string, `[contenteditable]` outside the stage. **None of the four carries corpus identity by itself; the filed list carried all four beside a genuine corpus read and so could not distinguish a dependency from a coincidence.**

**2.1.3 THE MASKING CONVENTION AND THE WINDOW RULE (adopted BY NAME from the pin, so the count is reproducible).** **The reader is the pin's own `maskCode` (`tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts`, `§0.15`) for the SOURCE-MASKING limb and `tokenizeCode` (`§0.16`) for the CODE-TOKEN limb, both USED AS THEY STAND — no new reader.** **THE RULE, stated as three clauses:**

1. **COMMENTS ARE BLANKED FIRST** (`tokenizeCode` calls `stripComments`), so a token that appears only in the driver's own prose (`.live-corpus` in a comment, `data-document-id` inside an explanatory sentence) **cannot count** — this is the over-count the raw text pass produced.
2. **THE READ WINDOW IS THE BLOCK'S OWN BODY, RESOLVED BY BRACES, NOT BY A FIXED-LENGTH WINDOW** — `blocksRegion()`'s `BLOCK_ENTRIES` + `blockBody(name)`, and for a helper `helperOwnBody(name)`/`arrowOwnBody(name)`. **A fixed-char window is FORBIDDEN as a census reader** (the pin's own `§0.16` note records why: a fixed window reads `xxxx` for a token the driver really carries, and reads a neighbouring entry's tokens as the block's own).
3. **THE FIXED POINT:** the helper closure is iterated until no new helper and no new body is admitted (`2.1.4`); **the reader is deterministic because the admission rule is monotone (a block is marked once and stays marked)**.

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` `F-2` — THE MASK ALONE CANNOT EXCLUDE A PROSE MENTION; THE CALL-POSITION CLAUSE DOES.⟩** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the pin's own `tokenizeCode` (`tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts`): for a single/double-quoted literal LONGER than its own `CODE_TOKEN_MAX_LITERAL = 44` the whole literal is no longer "kept" as a token — but the reader still KEEPS every `[A-Za-z_$.\d]` character ("a CODE token, not prose") — so `uf_search_2`'s ~100-character result-string sentence leaves `rag.query` perfectly readable, and `stripComments` cannot touch it either (it is not a comment).** **THE READING OF RECORD IS `2.1.1-B`: the census reader applies the CALL-POSITION test IN ADDITION TO the mask, never the mask alone.** The mask's own limbs (comments blanked FIRST; a SHORT literal kept as a token) stand exactly as written, and clauses 2/3 are unchanged.

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` FINDING `F-3` — THE WINDOW READER MUST NOT BE THE PIN'S `scanBalanced`. RECORDED PIN-SIDE DEFECT; owner: the TestWriter; NOT FIXED BY THIS UNIT.⟩** **THE DEFECT:** the pin's `scanBalanced(src, start)` is entered at an offset with `quote = null` — it has NO knowledge of the lexical context at its entry position (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the scanner's own text), and **the driver's own prose carries apostrophes inside the bodies it must resolve** — `ufBlockThrowReason`'s template literal `` `the block's setup read threw (${msg}) …` `` and `ufEnsureEditFixture`'s own comment (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at both sites) — so the closing brace is swallowed and the scan **returns `-1`**. **MEASURED CONSEQUENCE (`RECORDED READING; measurer: the TestWriter's red-set author`): `ufBlockThrowReason` · `ufRunBlock` · `ufDriverFailureRows` · `ufEnsureEditFixture` · `ufSurfaceCensus` · `o0FolderRows` all read EMPTY through it — a PHANTOM offence in every arm built on it — and it also blocks the pin's own `extractFunctionSource('finish')`.** **A SECOND, INDEPENDENTLY-READ LIMB OF THE SAME DEFECT IS RECORDED BECAUSE IT MAKES THE WORKAROUND NECESSARY EVEN WHERE NO APOSTROPHE EXISTS: the scanner has NO REGEX-LITERAL handling (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`), so the `\(` inside `ufBlockThrowReason`'s own `nullishDeref` test regex permanently over-counts brace depth.** **THE WORKAROUND THIS UNIT'S READER USES, AND IT IS THE CONTRACTED WINDOW RULE OF CLAUSE 2: a COLUMN-0 BRACE WINDOW — the driver's own top-level layout IS the brace resolution, so the body ends at the first following line-level `}` (the reader named `ufBodyAtColumnZeroIn`/`ufBalancedRegionIn` in the red set), with no character count and no scanner.** **THE PRE-EXISTING PIN LINES STAY BYTE-UNTOUCHED BY THIS UNIT: the landed `scanBalanced` keeps every established caller's behaviour, and the repair (or the adoption of a template/regex-aware scanner) is the TESTWRITER'S, recorded at `§12` item `9` and `§11.2.5`.**

**⟨END OF THE `2026-10-03` ADDITIONS TO `2.1.3`.⟩**

**2.1.4 THE HELPER-CLOSURE RULE — TRANSITIVE TO A FIXED POINT, NO HOP LIMIT.** **A `BLOCKS` key is IN the census iff its OWN body (`blockBody`) or the transitive helper closure of that body carries a class-`M` or class-`S` token in code position.** The closure is **the transitive closure of the driver's own module-level call graph, followed to a FIXED POINT with NO HOP LIMIT** — `ufEnsureDocumentSurface` → `ufPaneSearch` → `#pane-search li[data-document-id]` is **three** levels and is IN; the block-only ("one level") rule is **DELETED**, because the pin's own reader is three-level and a one-level rule cannot reach it. **The named fixed point: no further module-level function is reachable from the block's body that has not already been visited.** A recursive or mutually-recursive helper pair terminates on the visited-set test, never on a depth counter.

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` `F-1` — WHAT MAY SEED THE CLOSURE IS `2.1.1-D`'s LIMB, AND ONLY THAT CHANGES HERE.⟩** **A helper seeds the closure iff its OWN body carries a corpus-IDENTITY read — a class-`M` call per `2.1.1-A`/`2.1.1-C`, or a class-`S` read that USES a corpus identity. A helper whose only corpus-shaped token is the EDIT SURFACE's marker read as an OPAQUE presence/agreement signal (`ufSurfaceCensus`, `ufEditSurfaceState`) seeds NOTHING, so a block that reaches only such a helper is OUT** — **the two subjects are `stage_boot_landing_diag` and `stage_multimount_reachability` (`§2.2`'s clause table, `§2.3` `P-11`)**. **EVERYTHING ELSE IN THIS SUBSECTION IS UNCHANGED: transitive, monotone, no hop limit, terminating on the visited set, never on branch reachability and never on a runtime trace.**

**⟨SUPERSEDED, KEPT VISIBLE — THE FOUR TOKEN PASSES EXACTLY AS FILED:⟩** **the procedure as first written read `scripts/live-drive.mjs` textually in four passes: (1) ENUMERATE THE KEYS from `const BLOCKS = { … }` with `BLOCKS_HEADER`'s two-space-indented `<name>: [async] (h) =>` entry form, yielding `100` keys — the count the pin freezes (`BLOCK_CENSUS_SET`, `BLOCK_CENSUS_DIGEST`); (2) RESOLVE EACH KEY'S BODY to the end of its entry (the next header line, or the region's close); (3) MARK EVERY BODY THAT CARRIES A CORPUS-READ TOKEN, where a "corpus-read token" was one of: `rag.list_documents` · `rag.get_document` · `rag.query` · `.live-corpus` · `data-document-id` · `data-folder-path` · `data-folder-label` · `provident.focus` with a `document`/`nodeId` target · `#pane-doc-nav` · `#pane-search li` · `[contenteditable]` read `inside the stage` (`#zone:main`) · `#page-edit-surface` / `the UF_PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID constant` · `[data-edit-surface]` · `#tab-strip .tab` with a `kind === 'document'` filter · `ufEnsureDocumentSurface(` · `ufEnsureEditFixture(` · `ufOpenDocumentById(` · `ufPaneSearch(` · `ufDocNavRow(` · `o0FolderRows(` · `o0ResetFolderState(` · `O0_DOCUMENT_SELECTOR` · `O0_FOLDER_SELECTOR`; and (4) CLOSE OVER THE HELPERS, **ONE LEVEL, to a fixed point**.** **WHY IT IS SUPERSEDED: pass (3) was a token list with no stated predicate (finding 2), pass (4) contradicted itself — "one level" and "a fixed point" cannot both be the rule (finding 5) — and neither pass adopted a masking convention, so the string-literal fragments in the list (`.live-corpus`, `data-document-id`) also matched the driver's own comments and over-counted (finding 3).**

**A block that reads the store only through a corpus-INDEPENDENT path is recorded as such, by name, in `§2.3`** — so the census's boundary is visible in both directions and the declaration's `corpusRead:false` arm has a named complement.

The superseded heading is kept visible in this sentence.⟩** **Applying `2.1.1`–`2.1.4` to the filed `49`-row table yields `48` keys (`uf_panes_1` is EXCLUDED, `§2.2`)** **⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 4 — THE FIGURE IS SUPERSEDED: `48` → `47`, from TWO stated exclusions — `uf_panes_1` (limb 2 fails on a bare `li` count inside a FRAME, `§2.2` row `21`) and `ujr1_journal` (its sole MCP read is `provident.get_journal`, which `§2.1.2` states in terms is NOT class `M`). **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, SECOND PASS, FINDING 1 — THE DISTINGUISHING RULE, STATED AS A CLAUSE OF THIS SUBSECTION SO ROW `30`'s ADMISSION AND ROW `21`'s EXCLUSION ARE DECIDED BY ONE RULE AND NOT BY TWO READINGS OF PROSE.⟩**

**`§2.2.1` THE TWO-TOKEN DISTINCTION (it is `§2.1.1`'s own predicate, restated as a test a census reader can implement): `A READ COUNTS IFF (a) its RESULT IS READ AS STORE CONTENT (class `M`), or (b) its SELECTOR OR READ ATTRIBUTE IS KEYED BY A CORPUS IDENTITY (class `S`), or (c) it sits in CODE POSITION inside the body of a helper whose OWN BODY carries (a) or (b) — reached transitively to the fixed point (`§2.1.4`). IT DOES NOT COUNT IFF its result is discarded (`M-null`: the bare-`rag.query` boundary), or its value is a FRAME property (a list COUNT inside a pane frame, a `getBoundingClientRect()` height, a `ul` presence, an `is-collapsed` class).`**

| The contested read | The limb it satisfies / fails | In or out |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`ufEnsureEditFixture`** (reached from census rows `28`, `29`, `30`, `34`) | **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the function's OWN body: it WRITES `.live-page-edit-fixture.md` and `edit.import_markdown`s it (`selfProvisioning`'s carve-out — an INPUT of its own), AND in the `minBlocks > 1` branch it calls `rag.list_documents` (class `M`, result read as a document LIST: `all.documents.map((d) => d.documentId)`) and then `ufOpenDocumentById(h, id)` (a class-`H` seed) over `list.slice(0, 20)`. THE BRANCH'S CONDITION IS READ FROM THE ARGUMENT (`minBlocks > 1`), SO THE READ IS PART OF THE HELPER'S OWN CONTRACT, NOT DEAD CODE.** | **IN — limb (c) via (a), for every row that reaches the helper (its `minBlocks` default is `1`, so the closure is entered by every caller; the class-`M` token is in the helper's OWN body regardless of the runtime branch)** |
| **`paneOf` inside `uf_panes_1`** (census row `21`, filed) | **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the block's OWN body: `f.querySelectorAll('li').length` inside `#pane-doc-nav`, beside `getBoundingClientRect()` height, `!!f.querySelector('ul')` and `classList.contains('is-collapsed')`. The `li` value flows to `after.li === before.li` (a SELF-COMPARISON) and `mid.li === 0` (the collapse itself); the verdict is `headerOnly && reExpanded && sameRoot`.** | **OUT — a FRAME property. No corpus identity is read; with an empty corpus the assertion reads `0 === 0` and the verdict is unchanged.** |

**CONSEQUENCE FOR THE COUNT (`derived`, the rule named here): `49` filed ordinals − `uf_panes_1` (row `21`, out) − `ujr1_journal` (row `7`, out) = `47` census keys. THE SAME RULE IS WHAT MAKES ROWS `28`/`29`/`30`/`34` CENSUS ROWS, so the `47` and that membership cannot be read apart.**

**⟨THE `o0_*` NOTE, CARRIED FROM THE RULING.⟩** The five `o0_*` keys read a corpus-derived SURFACE (the doc-nav's
folder/document rows) while the **O-0 measurement/report MECHANISM** is a different object and is **NOT** declared
obsolete by the ruling. **This unit therefore puts the `o0_*` keys' corpus surface IN the census and leaves the O-0
mechanism EXACTLY as it is** (`§1.2`, `§9`). **The ruling text's own scope sentence is what licenses that split**
(`DECIDED: MOCK-DATA-SETS-AND-A-LAUNCH-SELECTION-ARG` clause (3)).

by this filing (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`) and each names what it DOES read instead.

## 3. THE OBSOLETE ROUTE — NAMED, NEVER EXTENDED

**`DECIDED: MOCK-DATA-SETS-AND-A-LAUNCH-SELECTION-ARG` clause (3) rules the pre-existing corpus data sets and their
supply mechanisms OBSOLETE.** Unit A's obligation under that clause is **exactly two things and no more**:

1. **NAME IT.** Any new declaration text, comment or printed consequence that mentions the old supply mechanism must
   name it **obsolete** and must not present it as the fixture's foundation. **The landing pass MAY add such naming to
   the declaration's own comments; it may not delete or rewrite the route's code.**
2. **DO NOT BUILD ON IT.** The declaration's `fixtureName` names the **fixture**, not the mechanism that used to
   supply it: a declared dependency reads **`corpus-documents`** / **`corpus-query-results`** /
   **`corpus-document-tabs`** (a NAME of what is read), **never** `--seed` / `--strict-seed` / `--o0-corpus` /
   `seedCorpus` / `o0MarkdownTree`. **A declaration that names an obsolete supply mechanism as its dependency is a
   review finding**, because it would make the fixture's replacement (UNIT B's data sets) look like a regression of a
   retired route.

**Three facts about the route are recorded here as `VERIFIED-BY-READ` so no landing pass has to re-derive them, and
NONE of them authorises a change to it:**

| **R-1** | The route is still the ONLY supply: `main()` seeds via `seedCorpus(seedDir)` / `o0MarkdownTree(seedDir)` → `edit.import_markdown`, with `seedDir = opt.seed ?? join(ROOT, '.live-corpus')`, and removes `.live-corpus/` after the run unless `--connect`. |
| **R-2** | The flags still parse: `--seed=`, `--corpus-root=`, `--strict-seed`, `--o0-corpus=`, `--no-seed`, `--connect`. |
| **R-3** | **The ruling's O-0 carve-out is load-bearing for `§2.2`'s five `o0_*` rows**: the O-0 measurement/report mechanism is **NOT** obsolete, so a census entry naming an `o0_*` block's corpus surface **is not** an extension of an obsolete route. |

**WHAT UNIT A DOES NOT DO TO THE ROUTE:** it does not delete it, deprecate it with a warning, rename it, re-point it,
add a flag, remove a flag, or make the declaration depend on it. **The route's disposition is UNIT B's** (`§9` `4`).

---

## 4. THE DECLARATION — its shape, and the DERIVATION that checks it

### 4.1 The declaration's shape (one entry per censused block)

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, SECOND PASS, NON-BLOCKING FINDING 14 — THE ENTRY-COUNT EQUALITY IS STATED HERE, because the first amendment required a `surface` member per entry (and a `block` member per entry) without ever tying the DECLARATION'S SIZE to the census's size, which left "a partial declaration" failing only for the keys it happened to omit and never for the keys it omitted wholesale.⟩**

**`§4.1.0` THE DECLARATION'S ENTRY COUNT IS EQUAL TO THE CENSUS'S SIZE, AND THE EQUALITY IS TWO-DIRECTIONAL: `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION.length === |census-derived|` — every censused key has exactly one entry (`A-1`, the no-missing-declaration arm) AND every entry's `block` is a censused key (an entry for a key the derivation reads as corpus-FREE with `corpusRead:true` is `A-2`'s phantom; an entry whose `block` is not a `BLOCKS` key at all is `A-4`'s shape offence). **THE CONTRACTED FAIL-STATE FOR A PARTIAL DECLARATION IS THEREFORE NOT ONLY `A-1`'S PER-KEY ABSENCE: `A-1` FAILS ON THE FIRST MISSING KEY AND NAMES IT, WHILE A DECLARATION THAT OMITS A CONTIGUOUS RUN OF KEYS FAILS THE SAME ARM WITH THE SAME `length` MISMATCH PRINTED — so a reader can tell "one entry was deleted" from "the tail of the census was never declared" by the arm's OWN two figures (`declared entries` vs `census-derived keys`), which the arm MUST print.** **The entry count is `REPORTED` wherever a figure appears in this file (`§2.2`'s derived-row table sums to `47`); what settles it is this equality, evaluated by `A-1`/`A-4` on the landed literal.** **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` `F-1` — THE EQUALITY'S FIGURE IS NAMED AND IT IS `47`: `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION.length === 47` on the landed literal, because the census of record is `47` keys (`§2.2`'s clause table, `§15.8`). A landed declaration whose length is `45` is the TestWriter's derivation's figure (`§2.2`'s `45`-record) and must arrive with its FIVE NAMED exclusions; a length of `47` with the `A-1` ambiguity list non-empty is COMPLIANT and the ambiguity list is the review's subject, never the arm's failure.⟩**

**A module-level constant — proposed name `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION` — exports an ARRAY of entries, ONE ENTRY PER
CENSUSED `BLOCKS` KEY.** The pin already reads exported literals out of the source (`extractExportedLiteral`), so the
constant must be **pure data (no function, no template, no computed key)** exactly as `MATRIX_ROWS`/`ROW_EXTENDED` are.

| Member | Type | Required | Meaning (each clause is a fail-state subject in `§4.2`) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `block` | string | **yes** | **The `BLOCKS` key.** Must exist in the parsed `BLOCK_ENTRIES` census; must be unique across entries. |
| `corpusRead` | boolean | **yes** ⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 2/5 — REPLACES the filed `fixtureDependency` union; the superseded row is kept below⟩ | **`true` iff the derivation (`§2.1.1`–`§2.1.4`) reads the block's OWN body (plus its transitive helper closure) as carrying a corpus-DEPENDENT read.** **It is a DERIVED CLAIM, not a label**: the arm requires the entry's value and the derivation's reading to AGREE in both directions. |
| `selfProvisioning` | boolean | **yes** ⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 2/6 — the carve-out that makes the park gate honest⟩ | **`true` iff the block WRITES its own corpus document (or its own edit fixture) and imports it in the same body** — the named cases are `boot_landing`, `import1`, `ms_store`, the seven `u_edit_1_live_*` keys other than `u_edit_1_live_commit_failure_warning`, and `u_edit_1_live_package_table_limitation`'s candidate scan. **A `selfProvisioning:true` entry is NEVER gated by the fixture-absent park branch** (its input is its own; parking it on an empty store would be a false park and would contradict `§5.2`'s `SELF/FM` rows). |
| `fixtureName` | string | **yes** | **The fixture the block READS, NAMED as *what is read*, never as the mechanism that supplies it** — the closed value set is **`'corpus-documents'`** · **`'corpus-query-results'`** · **`'corpus-document-tabs'`** · **`'self-provisioned-document'`** (a `selfProvisioning:true` entry) · **`'none'`** (a `corpusRead:false` entry). **A value naming `--seed` / `--strict-seed` / `--o0-corpus` / `seedCorpus` / `o0MarkdownTree` is a review finding (`§3` clause 2, `D-3`).** |
| `rows` | string[] | **yes** | **The block's DECLARED row id(s)**, each of which must appear in `MATRIX_ROWS`, `ROW_EXTENDED` or `COVERED_ROW_BLOCKS` (`ufDeclaredRowsForBlock`'s three sources — **the driver MINTS no id**). An EMPTY array is admissible **only** for a block carrying no declared row id in any of the three sources, and is then the park's no-id route (`§5.3`'s amendment). **The declared set must EQUAL `ufDeclaredRowsForBlock(block)`'s id set — an empty array on a block the tree DOES pair with a row id is `D-4`.** |
| `surface` | string ⟨the filed member, kept; see the naming clarification below⟩ | **yes** | The corpus-derived surface, in words, as `§2.2`'s table states it (e.g. *"the doc-nav document rows (`#pane-doc-nav [data-document-id]`)"*). **This member is what makes a park reason DERIVABLE at the site rather than improvised.** |
| **⟨SUPERSEDED, KEPT VISIBLE — THE FILED `fixtureDependency` ROW:⟩** | `'corpus-documents' \| 'corpus-query-results' \| 'corpus-document-tabs' \| 'none'` | as filed | **"The FIXTURE the block depends on, NAMED — what the block READS, never the mechanism that supplies it. `'none'` is admissible only for an entry that the derivation's phantom arm has also read as corpus-free."** **WHY IT IS SUPERSEDED: one member carried TWO orthogonal claims (does the block read a corpus at all / which fixture does it name), so the phantom arm had no independent axis to compare against and the park gate could not tell a self-provisioning block from a corpus-dependent one.** **The filed value names are NOT lost: the four read kinds are now the value set of `fixtureName`.** |

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 NON-BLOCKING FINDING 11 — THE `surface` MEMBER'S SOURCE, STATED SO IT CANNOT BE READ AS A SECOND PRINTED SURFACE.⟩** **The filed text named `opts.surface` (the object handed to `rowResult`) as "the printed source" of this member. THAT IS REFUTED AS A DESCRIPTION: the driver's `surface` is TWO different things and neither is this member's source — (a) the §6.1 row-result member `surface = { target, liveSurfacePresent }`, built by `ufSurfaceTarget(h)` and printed on the `ROW` line as `target=… liveSurfacePresent=…`; and (b) the DECLARATION's `surface` STRING, authored by this unit's landing pass.** **THE RULE: the declaration's `surface` is a HAND-AUTHORED PROSE MEMBER of the declaration entry (the same class of text as §2.2's table cell); the printed `surface={target,liveSurfacePresent}` object keeps its own source and its own contract (`§6.1`), and the two must not be conflated. The park reason QUOTES the declaration's `surface` STRING (`§5.1` clause 3).**

**THE DECLARATION REPLACES THE HAND-LIST AS THE RUNNER'S TEST, NOT AS A SECOND LIST.** The block runner's
fixture-absent branch is gated by **the DECLARATION's own predicate** in place of
`UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS.includes(n)`. **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 6 — THE GATE IS NAMED EXACTLY, because "membership in the declaration" is not the same test as "corpus-dependent": the branch fires iff `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION` holds an entry for the block with `corpusRead === true` AND `selfProvisioning === false`.** **A `corpusRead:false` entry (`user6_search_no_flicker`) and a `selfProvisioning:true` entry (`boot_landing`, the `u_edit_1_live_*` self-provisioners) are NOT gated** — the block runs and reports its own verdict (`§5.2`'s `SELF/FM` rows say exactly this). **The filed clause "membership in `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION`'s `block` set" is SUPERSEDED and kept visible above as written.** **`UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS` is not deleted and not weakened**: it is
**kept as the historical set the derivation must RECONCILE against** (`§4.2` arm `A-3`), so the movement from
hand-list to declaration is visible in the artifact and the movement is on the record.

**⟨SIXTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` (`§18.7`) — THE LANDED GATE IS NOT THE BOOLEAN PREDICATE ALONE: THE ABSENCE TEST IS PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE, AND ITS TWO PROBE LIMBS ARE WRITTEN HERE SO `§4.1` AND `§5.1` CLAUSE 1 DESCRIBE THE GATE AS IT LANDED (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `ufRunBlock`'s own fixture-absent branch and at `ufBlockPrecondition`/`ufFixturePreconditionRead`, `reader: this sixth amendment pass`).** **THE GATE, AS ONE PREDICATE — `GATE FIRES ⇔ pre && pre.present !== true && (the block's DECLARED fixtureName own probe RESOLVED true && that probe's present !== true) && entry.corpusRead === true && entry.selfProvisioning === false`.** **LIMB (i) `pre` = THE RUN-WIDE PRE-BLOCK PRECONDITION READ** (`ufBlockPrecondition`, one `rag.list_documents`, `present = documents !== null && documents > 0`, `kind` = `fixture-missing` under `--no-seed` else `empty-corpus`) — whose `present !== true` is the FIRST conjunct; **LIMB (ii) `ownProbe` = THE BLOCK'S OWN DECLARED FIXTURE'S PROBE** (`ufFixturePreconditionRead(h, opt, entry.fixtureName)`), a **REGISTRY LOOKUP BY `fixtureName`** (`UF_DECLARED_FIXTURE_PROBES[fixtureName] ?? null`, never a constant probe), whose own TWO limbs are `ownProbe.resolved === true` (the probe RAN and was READABLE — a registry MISS is `resolved:false` and parks NOBODY) and `ownProbe.present !== true` (that fixture's own reading is absent). **BOTH LIMBS ARE CONJUNCTS: a block RUNS when EITHER probe reports present, and PARKS only when the run-wide read AND its own declared fixture's probe BOTH read absent.** **THE THREE PROBE AXES THE REGISTRY DOCUMENTS (`UF_DECLARED_FIXTURE_PROBES[<fixtureName>]`), stated as the three values `ownProbe` can take and what each does to the gate:** **(1) `settles` — THE PROBE'S `read` SETTLES THAT FIXTURE'S OWN ABSENCE** (`'corpus-documents'` · `'corpus-query-results'` · `'corpus-document-tabs'` each carry `read: 'rag.list_documents'` at this head, so the three corpus fixtures' ABSENCE is settled by the SAME store read while their `unsettled` halves DIFFER) ⇒ `present !== true` ⇒ THE GATE MAY FIRE; **(2) `unsettled` — WHAT THE PROBE CANNOT DECIDE IS NAMED AND NEVER INFERRED**: `'corpus-query-results'`'s `unsettled` reads *whether a query actually PAINTS a result row for a given term* (a post-gesture read that cannot be taken before the block runs) and `'corpus-document-tabs'`'s reads *whether a document tab is actually OPEN in the strip at the block's own start* (the strip state is the block's own subject) ⇒ **AN `unsettled` HALF IS A `§5.5` `F-2` SUBJECT FOR UNIT B, NEVER A PARK GROUND**; **(3) `read: null` — THE FIXTURE HAS NO PROBE AND THE GATE CANNOT FIRE** (`'self-provisioned-document'` and `'none'`: `settles` is *nothing* and `unsettled` is `null`; a registry MISS returns `present:null, resolved:false` with the `declared-fixture-unprobed` extra) ⇒ **NEVER PARKED, AND NEVER INFERRED AS ABSENT**. **THE CONSEQUENCE, STATED SO NO READER INFERS A UNIVERSAL PARK: THE GATE'S POPULATION IS `34` DECLARED KEYS (`§16.3`) AND ITS FIRING IS CONDITIONAL ON TWO LIVE PROBES PER BLOCK — a run whose store is non-empty at a block's start gates NOBODY, which is exactly why `§8.3` item 1's acceptance reading is RE-STATED as the DERIVED OBSERVED SPLIT (`§18.2`).**

### 4.2 THE CHECKABILITY — the arms, each with a named mutation, and the reconciliation arm

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 2/5/13 — THE ARM PROCEDURE IS THE `§2.1` PREDICATE, NOT A TOKEN PASS, AND `§8.2` USES THIS ONE LABEL SET `A-1`…`A-8`.⟩**

**The declaration must be checkable BY DERIVATION, not by a token list.** **THE ARMS ARE LABELLED `A-1`…`A-8` AND `§8.2`'S TABLE USES THESE SAME LABELS (finding 13: the filed `§8.2` numbered its arms `1`…`8` beside these `A-` labels — one label set, stated here).** **Each arm names the MUTATION that must turn it RED.**

| Arm | What it derives, and from where | It FAILS when … | The NAMED MUTATION that must turn it red |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`A-1` — THE CENSUS ARM (no missing declaration)** | **Rebuild the census from the driver source by `§2.1.1`'s predicate, applied through `§2.1.2`'s read-grammar, read in CODE position under `§2.1.3`'s masking convention (`maskCode`/`tokenizeCode` + the block's OWN brace-resolved body), with `§2.1.4`'s TRANSITIVE-TO-FIXED-POINT helper closure.** Then require **every censused key to be present in `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION`**. | **a corpus-reading block is ABSENT from the declaration** | **REMOVE one entry** from the declaration literal — e.g. the `repro_dup_para` entry — and the arm must name it. A second generator **REMOVES the whole constant** and the arm must fail on the absence, never pass vacuously. **A THIRD generator (the masking tooth, finding 3) PLANTS a token in a COMMENT ONLY** (`// reads #pane-doc-nav [data-document-id]`) and the arm must NOT admit the block — a raw text pass admits it and is red. |
| **`A-2` — THE NO-PHANTOM ARM (no declared corpus read without a corpus read)** | For every declaration entry, **derive whether the block's OWN body plus its TRANSITIVE helper closure (`§2.1.4`) carries a corpus-dependent read**; require **the derivation and the entry to agree**: an entry with `corpusRead:true` must be read as corpus-reading, and an entry with `corpusRead:false` must be read as corpus-free. | **a declaration entry claims `corpusRead:true` and the source derivation reads the block as corpus-FREE** (the phantom direction — the ONLY direction this arm tests, because the opposite direction is `A-1`'s) | **SET `corpusRead:true` ON THE `user6_search_no_flicker` ENTRY** — the named case at this head, and the DECLARATION's own defect (`V-2`'s body read; the key is NOT in the landed hand-list, so the arm is exercised by the mutation, not by history). A second generator **STRIPS a corpus read out of a genuinely-declared block's body** (e.g. replacing `rag.get_document` in a body with a helper-free read) and the arm must fire, proving it reads the SOURCE and not the entry's own claim. |
| **`A-3` — THE RECONCILIATION ARM (the movement is on the record)** | Read the historical `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS` literal and the new declaration, and print/assert the **three sets**: `historical` · `census-derived` · their differences (`historical-minus-census` = a hand-listed key that is not corpus-dependent — **EMPTY at this head, `H \ C = ∅`**, `§16.1`; `census-minus-historical` = the undeclared reads, **`30`**). | **the historical set and the declaration are reconciled silently** — i.e. the run carries no readable statement of which keys ENTERED and which LEFT | **ADD `uf_panes_1` TO THE HAND-LIST literal** (⟨FOURTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`: THE FILED MUTATION `RE-ADD boot_landing` IS RETIRED — `boot_landing` IS A CENSUS MEMBER, so re-adding it leaves `historical-minus-census` EMPTY and the limb cannot fire (`§16.2`/`§16.8`); `uf_panes_1` is the driver's own census-EXCLUDED key and IS the case the limb detects⟩) and **DELETE a genuinely-declared entry** (the arm must show it in `census-minus-declared`). |
| **`A-4` — THE DECLARATION-SHAPE ARM** (`§4.3` `D-4`…`D-7`) | unique `block`; non-empty `fixtureName`/`surface`; `corpusRead`/`selfProvisioning` present and boolean; every `rows` id present in the tree's three sources; the literal statically evaluable | an invented row id, a duplicate key, a blank member, a non-pure literal, a `rows` array that omits an id the tree carries | inject an invented row id (`UF-MINTED-BY-THE-DRIVER`); duplicate an entry; blank a `surface`. (This is `§8.2`'s filed arm `4`, now on the one label set.) |
| **`A-5` — THE RUNNER-MEMBERSHIP ARM** | the block runner's fixture-absent branch tests **the DECLARATION's own predicate** (`corpusRead === true && selfProvisioning === false`), and the run-wide fixture-state member has **exactly one read** feeding **both** print sites (`§6.1`'s amendment) | the branch still tests the hand-list alone; the gate omits the `selfProvisioning` limb; a second, independent computation appears at either print site | revert the gate to `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS.includes(n)`; delete the `selfProvisioning === false` limb; duplicate the fixture-state expression at the second site |
| **`A-6` — THE PARK-NAMING ARM** (`§5.1` clause 3) | the park reason expression names the declaration's `fixtureName` **and** `surface` **and** the block, at the park site | the reason is the generic absence text, or names only the block | replace the reason with a generic string; drop the `surface` interpolation |
| **`A-7` — THE NO-APP-FAIL ARM** (`§7` clauses 1–2) | a precondition-kind reason still maps to `PARKED` + `preconditionFailed:true` + `realInput:false`, and the pin's existing `R1`/`R3` falsifiability/proxy arms stay green | a precondition is promoted to `FAIL`; a proxy turns into a PASS | empty `preconditionKinds` (the pin's own existing generator); flip a `proxyPASS` row to `pass:true` |
| **`A-8` — THE RUN-WIDE FIXTURE-STATE ARM** (`§6`, as amended) | the member exists; it prints at **the launch line, the summary, AND both early-abort paths**; every site reads **one** member; it is **not** inside the O-0 branch; it states the consequence and the `none`-form value | the field is absent; the field is absent from an early-abort path; it is scoped to `o0_*`; it is consequence-less | delete the summary-site print; delete the `--groups=` refusal's print; delete the module-level `main().catch` print; move the whole statement inside the `o0Blocks.length` branch |

**`A-9` — THE RUN-WIDE SCOPE LIMB (why this is not `--block=`-space-only).** ⟨renumbered `2026-10-02` from the filed `A-4`; the filed label is kept visible here so no reader loses the citation⟩ Every arm above is a **source
derivation**, so it holds for **`--block=all` and for every scoped invocation identically**; and the *runner* limb —
that the fixture-absent branch is gated by the DECLARATION's predicate — is asserted **on the runner's own source**,
not on one run's output (`§8.2` `A-5`). **An arm that could be satisfied by a scoped run's output alone is not this
unit's arm** (`§1.3`).

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` `F-1`/`F-2` — THE ARM EXPECTATIONS RE-DERIVED SO THE RULING REACHES THE ARM AND NOT ONLY THE PROSE. THE ARM COUNT, THE LABELS AND EVERY REGISTER TERM ARE UNCHANGED (`§11.2.2`: the register counts ATTEMPTS; the census figure is not a register term).⟩**

THE `6` OF THIS CELL STAYS A `REPORTED` LOWER BOUND AND MUST NOT BE CITED AS THE COUNT; `A-4`'s own print is the figure of record.**⟩ **THE FIGURE IS `REPORTED` AND ITS LABEL IS `REPORTED`, NOT `derived`: this pass's enumeration of the declared sources was not exhaustive (two of its source reads dropped entries the sources demonstrably carry, e.g. `uf_layout_2`/`uf_hist_4` in `COVERED_ROW_BLOCKS`), so the `6` is a LOWER BOUND this pass READ, never a count it derived. ## 5. THE PARK DISCIPLINE AND `RCA-11` CLAUSE (b)

### 5.1 The park rule (the contract, in eight clauses)

1. **A BLOCK WHOSE DECLARED FIXTURE IS ABSENT PARKS BY NAME.** **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 6 — THE GATE AND THE PRINTED FORM ARE NAMED EXACTLY; the filed clause is kept at the end of this item.⟩** The branch fires when the per-block precondition read (`ufBlockPrecondition`, `rag.list_documents` returning `>= 1` document) reports **`present !== true`** **AND** the block's declaration entry carries **`corpusRead === true`** **AND `selfProvisioning === false`**. **`selfProvisioning:true` BLOCKS ARE NOT PARKED** — their input is their own write+import, and parking them on an empty store would be a false park (`§5.2`'s `SELF/FM` rows and `§5.4` `OB-1`). **⟨SIXTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` (`§18.7`) — THE GATE THIS CLAUSE DESCRIBES IS THE **LANDED** GATE, WHICH IS NOT THE DECLARATION'S BOOLEAN PREDICATE ALONE: the branch also requires the block's **OWN DECLARED FIXTURE'S PROBE** to have RESOLVED and to read absent — `pre && pre.present !== true && (ownProbe.resolved === true && ownProbe.present !== true)` with `ownProbe = ufFixturePreconditionRead(h, opt, entry.fixtureName)` — so a run whose store holds ANY document at a block's start does NOT park that block even though its entry is `corpusRead:true && selfProvisioning:false`. THE TWO PROBE LIMBS AND THE THREE PROBE AXES (`settles` · `unsettled` · `read: null`) ARE WRITTEN AT `§4.1`'s sixth-amendment clause; THE `selfProvisioning` LIMB EXEMPTS A BLOCK FROM **PARKING** ONLY, NEVER FROM THE CENSUS (`§2.1.1-A`) AND NEVER FROM ITS OWN VERDICT (`§6.4`'s population table).⟩** **⟨SUPERSEDED, AS FILED:⟩** *"…and the block is in the declaration with a non-`'none'` `fixtureDependency`"*.
2. **THE PARK CARRIES ITS DECLARED ROW ID(S), from the TREE.** The ids come from `ufDeclaredRowsForBlock(block)`'s
   three declared sources (`MATRIX_ROWS` · `ROW_EXTENDED` · `COVERED_ROW_BLOCKS`) — **never an invented id, never a
   driver-minted id, never a `--block=`-local invention**. **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 6 — THE NO-ID CASE'S PRINTED FORM IS THE DIAGNOSTIC LINE, NOT A `ROW` LINE; the filed `diagResult` clause is the CORRECT one, and `§5.3` is amended to match.⟩** **A block carrying NO declared id falls back to `ufDriverFailureRows`' `diagResult` path: the result is `{diagnostic:true, row:null, dclass:null, realInput:false, pass:false}`, its `evidence` carries the precondition MARKER (`PRECONDITION-FAILED`), the block key and the reason text, and its `park` member is NOT `true` — so it prints as `DIAG  <label> <detail>` and NOT as `ROW  … verdict=PARKED parkReason=…`.** **THE JUSTIFICATION, STATED: the row-id carry is what `PARKED` means in this driver (`buildReportRow` prints `parkReason` only when `r.park === true`, and the no-id fallback is a diagnostic by construction); printing a `ROW` line with `row=null` would either fabricate a row id — forbidden by this very clause — or put a non-row into the matrix scope. The fixture absence is NAMED on the `DIAG` line and in the `PARKED`-adjacent `DRIVER-FAILURE`/`PRECONDITION-FAILED` record, never silently.** **The three census keys this head reaches through the no-id path are named in `§2.3` `P-10`.** **⟨FOURTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`: THE SENTENCE ABOVE STANDS FOR ITS THREE SUBJECTS AND IS INCOMPLETE AS A STATEMENT OF THE ROUTE'S POPULATION — the LANDED declaration puts `rows: []` on `15` of its `47` entries, `12` of them GATED, so `12` gated keys reach the no-id route at this head and the `12` are NAMED in `§16.7`; the printed form is the `DIAG` line for those and the `ROW … verdict=PARKED … parkReason=` line for the other `22` gated keys (`§16.7`, `§16.9` items 4/5). **⟨SIXTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` — THE FOURTH AMENDMENT'S `16`/`13`/`21` FIGURES OF THIS SENTENCE ARE SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE IN IT: the driver's own measurements moved them (`tabs` carries `rows: []` and is in NO declared-row source), so the reading of record is `15` empty-`rows` entries — `12` GATED + `3` never-gated — and the gated route split is `22` `ROW`-line + `12` `DIAG`-line (`§18.3`, `§16.7`).⟩**⟩**
3. **THE `parkReason` NAMES THE FIXTURE.** The reason string must contain, at minimum: **the fixture NAME from the
   declaration's `fixtureName`** ⟨amended `2026-10-02`: member renamed with the `§4.1` amendment; the filed member name `fixtureDependency` is kept visible here⟩, **the `surface` from the declaration**, **the block key**, and **the read that
   failed with its observed value** (`rag.list_documents -> <n> document(s)` or the `isError`/transport text verbatim).
   **A park whose reason does not name the fixture is a review finding** — the pre-existing generic text
   (*"the seeded corpus is ABSENT"*) is **not sufficient** under `RCA-11` clause (b) because it does not say **which
   fixture** and therefore cannot be re-derived as fixture-missing vs structural.
   **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, SECOND PASS, FINDING 11 — THE REASON PATH IS CONTRACTED BY NAME, WITH ITS INHERITED SITE NAMED. THE GAP THE FIRST AMENDMENT LEFT: it required the reason to name the fixture WITHOUT naming the member that carries it, the site that builds it, or the sentinel that substitutes when a site records none. THE CONTRACT, in five limbs (each `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the site named):⟩**
   - **(i) THE REASON IS BUILT AT THE PARK SITE, FROM THE DECLARATION ENTRY — `ufRunBlock`'s fixture-absent branch calls `ufDriverFailureRows(n, { kind: pre.kind, detail: pre.detail, extra: … })`; inside `ufDriverFailureRows` the reason string is composed as `` `${reason.kind}: ${reason.detail} (${reason.extra ?? 'no further detail'})` `` and stamped on the row's `parkReason` member (spread into the row's `extra`, gated on `d.preconditionFailed`). **THE LANDING PASS MUST PUT THE DECLARATION'S `fixtureName` AND `surface` INTO `reason.extra` AT THAT CALL SITE** — the branch's `extra` text is the ONLY carrier: `ufBlockPrecondition`'s own `detail` is `"rag.list_documents -> <n> document(s)"` (or the `isError`/transport text) and names no fixture.
   - **(ii) THE ROW CARRYING IT:** the gated branch's rows come from `ufDriverFailureRows`, whose id-carrying branch stamps `park: d.preconditionFailed` (an ARRAY, `driverFailureReason` returns `preconditionFailed: true` for a precondition kind), so the row's `park === true` and its `parkReason` is its own.
   - **(iii) THE RESOLUTION SITE IS `buildReportRow`, AND THE SENTINEL IS `UF_NO_PARK_REASON`:** `const parkedReason = r.park === true ? UF_NO_PARK_REASON : null` and `r = { ...r, parkReason: r.parkReason ?? parkedReason }` — so **a row that parks with NO recorded reason is not skipped: it carries the NAMED sentinel `UF_NO_PARK_REASON` (whose literal at this head is `'(no parkReason recorded)'`), and `UF_NO_PARK_REASON` is the ONE place the substitution happens.** **The printed `ROW` line reads `parkReason=` only when the row's own `park === true` (`const parkText = r.park === true ? … : ''`), printing `r.parkReason ?? UF_NO_PARK_REASON`.**
   - **(iv) THE NO-ID DROP IS THE ONE PLACE THE REASON IS NOT A FIELD:** `ufDriverFailureRows`' `diagResult` limb carries the marker and the block in its TEXT (`evidence`/`detail`), which is why the `DIAG` line prints the reason and the `ROW` line never exists for it (`§5.3`'s amendment). **`parkedReason` does NOT apply to it: `diagResult` carries no `park` FIELD AT ALL, so `r.park === true` is `false`, so `buildReportRow` resolves `parkedReason` to `null` and `ufPushRows` DROPS the result before any `buildReportRow` is reached (`(vi)` below).**
   - **(v) A PARKED ROW MAY NOT SILENTLY LOSE ITS REASON, and the TestWriter's assertions follow exactly these fields: on a `ROW` line, assert `park === true` AND `parkReason` — a `string` if the site recorded one, the `UF_NO_PARK_REASON` sentinel otherwise; on a `DIAG` line, assert `park` is ABSENT, `parkReason` is ABSENT, and the marker text (`PRECONDITION-FAILED` + the kind) is IN `evidence`/`detail`.** **The `UF_NO_PARK_REASON` sentinel is INHERITED (`RECORDED READING`, measurer: the landed live-driver pin's `C-5` limb), not introduced by this unit; this unit's own addition is that the reason must NAME THE FIXTURE.**
   - **(vi) ⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 6 — THE `ufPushRows` FILTER, CONTRACTED EXPLICITLY.⟩** **`ufPushRows`'s first statement is `if (typeof res.row !== 'string') continue`. SO: THE `PRECONDITION-FAILED` MARKER IS PRESENT IN THE ROW SET **iff** THE ROW CARRIES A DECLARED ROW ID; THE `diagResult` FALLBACK (`row: null`) IS FILTERED OUT OF `reportRows` AND NEVER ENTERS THE COUNTERS, THE RECONCILIATION, THE EXIT PATH OR THE `§6.1` SUMMARY — while its `DIAG` line IS PRINTED (and counted as `diag` by `printBlockVerdict`, which dispatches `r.diagnostic === true` FIRST).** **THE ABSENCE FROM THE ROW SET IS INTENDED, AND HERE IS THE CONTRACTED STATEMENT OF IT: a block with no declared row id has no row for a marker to ride (`§5.1` clause 2's justification, unchanged), so the marker's carriage is by DESIGN `DIAG`-only for that block — but the absence must never be SILENT (`F-4`), so the `DIAG` line is a REQUIRED print and its absence is the offence.** **HOW A READER TELLS THE TWO APART, stated as a rule a TestWriter can assert: (a) IN THE ARTIFACT — a `ROW … verdict=PARKED … parkReason=` line means the block carried a declared row id; a `DIAG <label> <detail>` line whose detail contains `PRECONDITION-FAILED:` means the block carried none; (b) IN `reportRows` — the row-set member `row` is a non-empty `string` iff a declared id existed, so a `rows=[]`-shaped block leaves NO entry and a `reportRows.filter(r => r.block === n)` over such a block is EMPTY BY CONSTRUCTION; (c) A READER MUST NOT CONCLUDE "NO FIXTURE ABSENCE WAS REPORTED" FROM AN EMPTY `reportRows` FILTER — the DIAG line is where that block's absence is recorded, and the run's own `diag` counter includes it.**
4. **IT IS NEVER AN APP FAIL.** The verdict is `PARKED` (via `driverFailureReason`'s precondition kinds ⇒
   `verdict:'PARKED'`, `preconditionFailed:true`, `realInput:false`), **never `FAIL`, never `NOT-DRIVEN`, never a
   PASS**, and it never enters the app-layer pile. **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02` — THE NO-ID CASE IS THE ONE NAMED EXCEPTION, and it is an exception in the OTHER direction: it prints `DIAG` (a non-verdict), never a `FAIL` and never a `PASS`. A `DIAG` line is not an app FAIL and never enters the app-layer pile either.⟩**
5. **IT IS NEVER A SILENT `rows=0` READ.** A declared block must not execute its own setup read against an absent
   fixture and then report the emptiness as its own observation (a `rows=0` / `missing` / `zero-box` row result).
6. **A PARK IS LEGAL FOR THE STRUCTURAL CASE ONLY.** `RCA-11` clause (b) permits parking **a structurally
   non-exercisable surface** — an OS-owned dialog, a scope the MCP+CDP surface cannot reach — **with the recorded
   park reason**. **A park whose only ground is *"the fixture is missing"* is NOT legal under this unit**: it names a
   condition UNIT B removes, so it must be **an OPEN obligation on UNIT B** (`§5.4`), not a resting disposition.
   **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 10 — THE RECORDED DECISION THAT RECONCILES THIS CLAUSE WITH `§8.3` ITEM 1, because the filed pair contradicted itself. THE TWO CLAIMS ANSWER TWO DIFFERENT QUESTIONS AND BOTH STAND, NAMED: (a) *A fixture-missing park is the LEGAL AND REQUIRED ACCEPTANCE OUTCOME of a run whose fixture is absent* — `§8.3` item 1's reading, and `RCA-11` clause (b)'s "park ONLY … WITH the recorded park reason" read as honesty about the run; and (b) *a fixture-missing park may NOT be the UNIT's resting disposition* — it must be filed as an OPEN obligation on UNIT B (`OB-1`). **THE PER-ROW RESOLUTION: for every `FM` row, the park is legal as THAT RUN's outcome (with `parkReason` naming the fixture) AND the row is an OPEN `OB-1` obligation; the class is never "disposition settled".** **For an `SE` row, a park is legal as the row's disposition AND carries no `OB-` item.**⟩
7. **THE CLASSIFICATION IS DERIVED, NOT ASSERTED.** Every censused block's park reason is classified below (`§5.2`)
   as **`fixture-missing`** or **`structurally non-exercisable`**, and **the column "what settles it" names the
   observation that decides it** — so a later pass can falsify a classification instead of inheriting it.
8. **NOTHING HERE RE-OPENS A LANDED CLAUSE.** The park machinery (`§0.3` `V-6`), the `H-4` verdict table and the
   `F-6`/`F-7` discriminated reads **stand**; this unit changes **what the runner tests** (the declaration's own
   `corpusRead`/`selfProvisioning` predicate, not a hand-list) and **what the park reason says** (the named fixture,
   not a generic absence).

### 5.2 THE PER-ROW PARK-REASON CLASSIFICATION (every censused block)

**Legend.** `FM` = **fixture-missing** — the row is blocked solely because the corpus fixture is absent; **a park is
NOT a legal resting disposition and the row becomes an OPEN obligation on UNIT B** (which supplies the data set the
row needs). `SE` = **structurally non-exercisable** — the surface the row needs is outside the fixture's reach
(OS-owned, or a mechanism the app/mcp surface does not expose), so a park **with the recorded reason** is the legal
disposition. `SELF` = the block **provisions its own document** (writes a fixture file and imports it) and therefore
does not depend on a pre-existing corpus data set — **it is `FM` only for the IMPORT precondition** (`edit` group
enabled + `edit.import_markdown` registered) and for the multi-block fallback that reads `rag.list_documents`.

### 5.3 The park's printed shape (the record a reader gets)

**On the fixture-absent route the driver prints, per declared row id the block carries, the SAME `ROW` line form it
prints today** (`row` · `block` · `verdict=PARKED` · `dclass` · `realInput=false` · `surface` · `proxyPASS` ·
`gesturePath` · `failingClause` · `parkReason` · `evidence`), **plus the block-level `PARK` line**. **The two additions
this unit contracts are (a) the `parkReason` must carry the declaration's `fixtureName` NAME and `surface` text
(`§5.1` clause 3) ⟨amended `2026-10-02`, member renamed with `§4.1`⟩, and (b) a block carrying NO declared row id must still be named** in its `diagResult` reason
(`ufDriverFailureRows`' existing fallback), **so no censused block's fixture absence can end in an unnamed line.**

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 6 — THE NO-DECLARED-ROW-ID PRINTED FORM, AND THE RECONCILIATION WITH `§5.1` CLAUSE 2. the filed paragraph above is kept visible; this block is the contract of record for the no-id case.⟩**

**THE PRINTED FORM FOR A BLOCK WITH NO DECLARED ROW ID IS THE `DIAG` LINE — NOT A `ROW` LINE. `§5.1` clause 2 IS THE CLAUSE THAT GOVERNS, and the filed `§5.3` sentence claiming the printed `ROW` line carries `parkReason` "for such a block" was WRONG (`REFUTED-BY-READ`) and is superseded.** **THE EXACT FIELD SET of the no-id fallback result (`ufDriverFailureRows`' existing `diagResult` path, `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at this head):** **`row: null` · `assertion: null` · `dclass: null` · `realInput: false` · `evidence` = the `driverFailureReason` marker (carrying `PRECONDITION-FAILED` and the kind) plus the text *"the block carries no declared row id; its verdict is classified, never counted as an app FAIL"* · `proxyPASS: false` · `surface: {target:'assembled-renderer', liveSurfacePresent:null}` · `pass: false` · `diagnostic: true` · `detail` = the same text as `evidence`.** **THE THREE FIELDS THE CASE DOES **NOT** CARRY, stated so no TestWriter asserts them: NO `park` FIELD AT ALL (not `park: false` — the KEY IS ABSENT; `diagResult` builds its object from a fixed literal that names only `row`/`assertion`/`dclass`/`realInput`/`evidence`/`proxyPASS`/`surface`/`pass`/`diagnostic`/`detail` plus the caller's `extra`, and `diagResult(detail)` is called with NO `extra` at this fallback site), NO `parkReason`, NO row id.** **⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 5 — THE FIELD SET IS THE TREE'S, STATED AS AN ASSERTION TABLE SO NO HEDGE REMAINS. `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `diagResult` and at `ufDriverFailureRows`' no-id limb, `reader: this second pass`:⟩** **THE TESTWRITER MAY ASSERT, FOR A NO-ID FIXTURE-ABSENT RESULT, EXACTLY: `row === null` · `assertion === null` · `dclass === null` · `realInput === false` · `evidence` a non-empty `string` CONTAINING the `PRECONDITION-FAILED` marker AND the block key AND the reason text · `proxyPASS === false` · `surface` deep-equal to `{target:'assembled-renderer', liveSurfacePresent:null}` · `pass === false` · `diagnostic === true` · `detail === evidence` (same text) — AND IT MUST ASSERT `'park' in res === false` and `'parkReason' in res === false` (KEY ABSENCE, not a falsy value).** **FOR A NON-PARKED ROW FROM THE SAME BUILDER (`driverFailureReason` returning `preconditionFailed: false`, e.g. an `isError` kind), the row's `park` field is `false` (a present, falsy field) and `parkReason` is absent — so the two cases are DISTINGUISHED BY KEY PRESENCE, and that is the assertion a TestWriter writes.** **WHY (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`, the reason the filed contract was unimplementable): `buildReportRow` gates the printed park text on the row's OWN `park === true` (`const parkText = r.park === true ? …`), and `printBlockVerdict` dispatches `r.diagnostic === true` FIRST, printing `DIAG  <label> <detail>` and returning the `diag` counter. A `diagResult` therefore can never print a `ROW` line and can never print `parkReason=`.** **⟨THE FIRST AMENDMENT'S ONE IMPRECISE SENTENCE IS SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE: *"its `park` member is NOT `true` — so it prints as `DIAG`"* (`§5.1` clause 2). THE CORRECT READING: the `park` member is ABSENT, which makes `r.park === true` false — the same outcome by a stronger statement, and the stronger one is what an assertion must test.⟩** **WHY (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`, the reason the filed contract was unimplementable): `buildReportRow` gates the printed park text on the row's OWN `park === true` (`const parkText = r.park === true ? …`), and `printBlockVerdict` dispatches `r.diagnostic === true` FIRST, printing `DIAG  <label> <detail>` and returning the `diag` counter. A `diagResult` therefore can never print a `ROW` line and can never print `parkReason=`.** **WHAT THE NO-ID CASE OWES INSTEAD — and this is the whole of the contract: (i) the line NAMES the block and the `PRECONDITION-FAILED` marker in its detail text; (ii) the reason text names the fixture through the SAME `driverFailureReason` detail the id-carrying rows use; (iii) the block-level `DRIVER-FAILURE …` line (thrown-precondition route) and the `PRECONDITION …` line (declared-but-ungated route) stay as the driver prints them.** **THE TWO CLAUSES ARE NOW CONSISTENT: a no-id block is NAMED (never a silent line) WITHOUT being counted as a `PARKED` row — `F-4` is about a park whose id VANISHES, and the no-id case has no id to vanish.** **THE THREE KEYS THAT REACH THIS PATH AT THIS HEAD ARE NAMED (`§2.3` `P-10`).**

### 5.4 The OPEN obligations this classification RAISES (each named, owned, and NOT parked)

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02` — THE HEADING'S "THREE" IS SUPERSEDED: there are FOUR (`OB-0`…`OB-3`); the superseded figure is kept visible in the heading and the fourth item is added at the end of this subsection.⟩**

1. **`OB-1` — THE `FM` CLASS IS NOT A RESTING PARK.** Every `FM` row above is blocked by *absent fixture data*. Under
   `§5.1` clause 6 such a park **may not stand as the unit's disposition** — **⟨amended `2026-10-02`, finding 10: it IS the run's legal outcome, and it is not the disposition; the two claims are separated in `§5.1` clause 6's amendment⟩**. **Owner: UNIT B.** **UNIT B's data sets must name,
   per row, the document property the row needs** — the three explicitly-named requirements this census found are:
   **a document with a stored `<table>`** (`#35`), **a document with an inline element outside the decomposer's set**
