## 6. THE RUN-WIDE FIXTURE STATE — PRINTED ON EVERY RUN

### 6.1 The member, its site, and its exact shape

**A RUN-WIDE fixture state is part of the run's identity, printed ONCE per run — never per block, and never omitted.**

| Aspect | The contract |
| --- | --- |
| **The member** | a `launchProfile` member named **`fixtureState`**, holding a **string** — the human-readable fixture state — beside a machine-readable pair: **`fixtureKind`** (`'none'` \| `'mock-data-set'`) and **`fixtureId`** (the selected data set's NAME, or `'none'`). Under UNIT A (UNIT B not landed) the values are **`fixtureState: 'no fixture data set selected'`**, `fixtureKind: 'none'`, `fixtureId: 'none'`. |
| **Where it prints** | **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 9 — THE SITES ARE ENUMERATED SO "EVERY RUN" IS IMPLEMENTABLE; the filed two-site form is kept visible at the end of this row.⟩** **FOUR PRINT SITES, EVERY PATH THE DRIVER CAN EXIT BY, ONE VALUE: (1) the `LAUNCH PROFILE` line (as filed); (2) the run-summary line `[live-drive] §6.1 summary: …` (as filed); (3) THE `--groups=` EMPTY-VALUE ARG-REFUSAL PATH — which returns at `process.exitCode = 2` BEFORE the launch profile is built, so the field is printed on that path's own `ARG-REFUSED` line (the refusal must name the fixture state too, because a refusal is a reading about the run's identity); (4) THE MODULE-LEVEL `main(process.argv.slice(2)).catch(…)` ERROR PATH — which prints `[live-drive] ERROR: …` and sets exit `2` with no summary at all, so it prints the field in the SAME line as the error.** **THE ORDER IS PART OF THE CONTRACT ONLY IN ONE RESPECT: sites (1) and (2) must carry the SAME value and are asserted by source; sites (3)/(4) are asserted by the ARM `A-8` reading each early-exit path's own source, because no node row can run them.** **⟨SUPERSEDED, AS FILED:⟩** *"on the SAME `LAUNCH PROFILE` line … AND in the run's summary block — so a scoped run, a connect run and a full battery all carry it. TWO print sites, ONE value."* |
| **Its source** | **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDING 9 — THE FILED SOURCE `opt.fixture ?? 'none'` DOES NOT EXIST AT THIS HEAD; `REFUTED-BY-READ` at the driver's own `opt` initializer, which carries `mode`/`port`/`cdpPort`/`home`/`seed`/`corpusRoot`/`strictSeed`/`groups`/`emptyGroups`/`block`/`noSeed`/`keepHome`/`connect`/`gpu`/`o0Corpus`/`o0Out`/`display`/`cliArgs` — and NO `fixture` member (Unit A adds no arg; the selection arg is UNIT B's).⟩** **THE REAL SOURCE AT THIS HEAD IS A MODULE-LEVEL CONSTANT THE LANDING PASS DECLARES — `const UF_FIXTURE_STATE = { state: 'no fixture data set selected', kind: 'none', id: 'none' }` (pure data, `Object.freeze`-free, declared ONCE at module level, OUTSIDE `main()`), and the ONE READ of it that feeds every site is a single inline expression per site, written so a source reader can pin it: at the launch-profile site `fixture: UF_FIXTURE_STATE` inside the `launchProfile` object literal, and at the summary site `fixture: UF_FIXTURE_STATE` inside the `summary` object literal.** **⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 8 — THE EXACT ONE-READ EXPRESSION AND ITS FOUR SITES, IN THE FORM A NODE ARM READS (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` for the two sites' present shape, named below).⟩** **THE EXPRESSION, VERBATIM: `const UF_FIXTURE_STATE = { state: 'no fixture data set selected', kind: 'none', id: 'none' }` — ONE module-level literal, named once.** **ITS FOUR SITES, EACH ANCHORED TO THE LINE THAT PRINTS IT (no line numbers are cited, per this file's discipline):** **(1) inside the object literal assigned to `launchProfile` (the same literal that carries `mode`, `port`, `cdpPort`, `display`, `groups`, `effectiveGroups`, `noSeed`, `gpu`, `connect`, `block`) — the member read by the `LAUNCH PROFILE` print at its `JSON.stringify(launchProfile)` interpolation; (2) inside the object literal assigned to `summary` (the literal printed by the `[live-drive] §6.1 summary:` line) — **`VERIFIED-BY-READ`: at this head `summary` copies `launchProfile.mode`/`.port`/`.cdpPort`/`.groups`/`.effectiveGroups` MEMBER BY MEMBER and contains NO spread of `launchProfile` at all, so the `fixture` member MUST be written into the `summary` literal explicitly (`fixture: UF_FIXTURE_STATE`) — a `{...launchProfile}` rewrite is NOT the contracted form and is NOT required; what is contracted is that the value is the SAME OBJECT REFERENCE, never a recomputation**; (3) the `--groups=` EMPTY-VALUE REFUSAL's `ARG-REFUSED` print inside the `if (opt.emptyGroups)` guard (which `return`s with `process.exitCode = 2` BEFORE `launchProfile` exists); (4) the module-level `main(process.argv.slice(2)).catch(…)` handler's `[live-drive] ERROR:` print (which sets `process.exitCode = 2` and prints no summary).** **AT SITES (3)/(4) THE EXPRESSION IS `JSON.stringify(UF_FIXTURE_STATE)` (or the three members interpolated) — READ FROM THE SAME CONSTANT, NEVER RE-DERIVED.** **THE ONE-READ RULE IS WHAT MAKES THE FOUR SITES PROVABLY AGREE, and it is what `A-8` asserts: exactly ONE occurrence of the object literal `{ state: …, kind: …, id: … }` in the module scope, and every site reading THAT binding.** **`opt.fixture` is a FORWARD-LOOKING name that this head cannot honour; it is kept visible as superseded and is NOT to be written by the landing pass.** |
| **May it be omitted?** | **NO.** Not on `--no-seed`, not on `--connect`, not on a scoped `--block=`, not on a run with no `o0_*` block, **and not on either early-abort path (site `3`/`4` above)**. **An artifact with no fixture-state statement is INVALID under this unit** (`F-5`); **⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`: THE TWO EARLY-ABORT PATHS PRODUCE NO ARTIFACT AT ALL — they abort before any reading — so `X-1`'s "artifact INVALID" limb attaches to sites (1)/(2) only, and what the early-abort paths owe is the FIELD ON THEIR OWN LINE (the named early-abort clause added to `X-1`)⟩**. |
| **What it may NOT be** | **it may not be inferred from the O-0 branch's `corpusSource`** (that field is O-0-scoped and names the OLD supply mechanisms — `--o0-corpus`, `operator-store`, `seed` — which the ruling declares OBSOLETE, `§3`). **The two must not be conflated**: if the landing keeps `corpusSource`, it stays exactly where it is and is **not** the run-wide statement. |
| **Its consequence clause** | the printed line must state the **CONSEQUENCE**, in the same idiom the run's group line already uses for the effective-group set: *"a run whose fixture state is `none` reports every declared corpus-dependent block PARKED BY NAME (`PRECONDITION-FAILED`), so no corpus-shaped reading in this artifact may be quoted as a live-corpus reading"*. **⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` — THE CONSEQUENCE CLAUSE IS AMENDED: IT MUST BE **DERIVED FROM THE DECLARATION AND CONDITIONAL ON THE RUN**, AND IT MUST CARRY THE GATED POPULATION AND THE EXCLUDED ENGINE FAMILY IN SCOPE (`§6.5`, `§17.3`). A UNIVERSAL PRESENT-TENSE CLAIM IS **SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE HERE AS THE FILED FORM OF THIS CELL**: the sentence above asserts *"every declared corpus-dependent block PARKED BY NAME"* on every run, and at this head that universal is FALSE IN THE ARTIFACT — the driver's `LAUNCH PROFILE` consequence text is a constant string (`UF_FIXTURE_STATE_CONSEQUENCE`), and the seven `gnosis_*` keys are excluded from the census by name, carry no declaration entry, are never gated, YET read corpus-keyed DOM and emit real row verdicts `UF-GNOSIS-1`…`UF-GNOSIS-6` (`§2.3` `P-7`, `§17.3`). THE FILED SENTENCE'S SUBJECT — "`every declared` … block" — is ALSO the population the `LAUNCH PROFILE` line mislabels `17` for (`§16.9` item 3, `§17.5`).⟩** |

### 6.3 Why `none` is the correct value at this head (and why it is not a park)

**Unit B has not landed**, so no data set exists to select; the ruled form is *"a set of mock data sets plus an arg
that picks which one"*, and **neither member is Unit A's** (`§1.3`). **`none` is therefore a TRUE statement of the
fixture state, printed unconditionally — it is NOT a park, NOT an omission and NOT an excuse.** Its whole purpose is
`G-5`'s: a reading taken under no fixture is **attributable from the artifact alone** and **may never be quoted as a
live-corpus reading**.

### 6.4 ⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`⟩ THE ABSENCE TEST IS PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE — THE CONTRACT `§5.5` `F-2` NEEDS

**THE CLAUSE, ONE SENTENCE: THE PRE-BLOCK ABSENCE TEST FIXTURES A BLOCK IS GATED ON MUST BE THE BLOCK'S OWN DECLARED FIXTURE — a per-declared-fixture presence test over the `fixtureName` values of `§4.1`'s closed set — and NOT a single run-wide store probe whose non-emptiness is read as "every declared fixture is present".**

**THE POPULATION THE TEST MUST COVER, NAMED (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` of the landed declaration literal, key by key; the `fixtureName` value set is `§4.1`'s closed set, so the groups below ARE the four names):**

| `fixtureName` | The declared keys carrying it (`derived`: the landed literal, read by this pass) | What the test must read |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`'corpus-documents'`** | the bulk of the gated set — `tabs` · `toolbar_undo` · `v1_adjacency` · `v2_scoped` · `user4_main_editable` · `repro_nbsp` · `repro_dup_para` · `uf_tabs_1` · `uf_tabs_3` · `uf_tabs_4` · `uf_panes_12` · `uf_panes_12_diag` · `uf_hist_4` · `uf_hist_6` · `uf_layout_2` · `u_edit_1_live_commit_failure_warning` · `shell_integration` · `v3_docnav` · `stage_async_mount_race_v1` · `stage_doc_surface_precondition_diag` · `stage_docnav_switch_inside_async` · `stage_document_tab_paints_its_document` · `stage_foreign_rederive_v2` · `stage_refresh_survival_v5` · `stage_surface_census_i2r` (`7`) · the five `o0_*` keys (`o0_document_row` · `o0_folder_row` · `o0_gpu_control` · `o0_repeat_determinism` · `o0_track_ablation`) — **the group's membership is a `VERIFIED-BY-READ` NAMED SET, not a count; the count the gate needs is the declaration-derived `34` of `§16.3`, and the `7` `stage_*` + `5` `o0_*` sizes agree with `§16.7`'s gated enumeration** | the store document list (a document to open/focus/read) |
| **`'corpus-query-results'`** | **`uf_panes_14` · `uf_tabs_7` · `uf_tabs_7_diag`** — **every one of them `corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false`, i.e. GATED** | the pane search's PAINTED RESULT ROWS (`#pane-search li[data-document-id]`) — a query-result fixture, which a non-empty document list does NOT imply |
| **`'corpus-document-tabs'`** | **`user9_search_open_in_tab`** (`corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false`, GATED) | the rendered corpus DOCUMENT TAB rows (`#tab-strip .tab[data-document-id]`) |
| **`'self-provisioned-document'`** | the `10` `selfProvisioning:true` keys (`§16.4`)— **NEVER GATED** | nothing: the block supplies its own input; the test must not gate them — **⟨SIXTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`: THE GATE-vs-OWN-BODY DISTINCTION, STATED BECAUSE THIS ROW'S WORDS "NEVER GATED" ARE NOT THE WHOLE CLAIM: the `selfProvisioning:true` limb EXEMPTS THE BLOCK FROM THE **PARK GATE** ONLY — the block still RUNS, still carries `corpusRead:true` (it IS a census member, `§2.1.1-A`), and its own body is still subject to the ABSENCE TEST AS A READING (`§6.4` clause 1: no probe is registered for this name), so a `selfProvisioning:true` block's own body may STILL read an absent store and report that shape from its own verdict — which is a `§5.5` `F-2`-class outcome for UNIT B, never a park (`§5.4` `OB-1`). THE EXEMPTION IS FROM PARKING, NEVER FROM THE CENSUS AND NEVER FROM ITS OWN VERDICT.** |
| **`'none'`** | the `3` `corpusRead:false` keys (`§16.6`) — **NEVER GATED** | nothing: they carry their own verdict |

**THE FILED-OUT FORM, KEPT VISIBLE AND DATED (the tree's shape as this pass read it): THE LANDED `ufBlockPrecondition` IS ONE NON-EMPTY READ OF ONE TOOL** — `rag.list_documents` called once per block, with `present = documents !== null && documents > 0`, and `kind` set to `fixture-missing` (under `--no-seed`) or `empty-corpus` otherwise (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`). **SO WITH A STORE HOLDING ANY DOCUMENT, `present` is `true` for EVERY block, and a block declaring `corpus-query-results` or `corpus-document-tabs` RUNS against a declared-absent fixture** — the exact outcome `§5.5` `F-2` forbids ("a declared block executing its setup read against an absent fixture and reporting `rows=0`/a `missing`-path row"). **THE RULING: THE TEST IS PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE. `F-2` STANDS AS FILED AND IS NOT RELAXED.**

**THE TEST IS A PREDICATE, STATED SO A TESTWRITER CAN DERIVE EVERY STATE AND FAIL-STATE FROM IT:**
1. **For each `fixtureName` of the closed set, the driver carries a NAMED probe returning a presence verdict for THAT fixture's own surface** — the store document list for `'corpus-documents'`, the pane search's painted result rows for `'corpus-query-results'`, the rendered document-tab rows for `'corpus-document-tabs'`. **`'self-provisioned-document'` and `'none'` need no probe: no gated key carries them (`§16.4`, `§16.6`).**
2. **A GATED key is parked iff ITS OWN declared fixture's probe reads ABSENT** — never on another fixture's presence, and never on a run-wide count. A gated key whose own fixture is PRESENT runs and carries its own verdict; a gated key whose own fixture is ABSENT parks by name (`§5.1` clause 3's reason, `§5.5` `F-1`).
3. **FAIL-STATE (per-fixture): a gated key executes while its own declared fixture's probe reads absent** ⇒ `§5.5` `F-2` is committed, and `§8.2` `A-5`'s predicate reading is red. **FAIL-STATE (crossed): a gated key parks on a DIFFERENT fixture's absence** ⇒ a FALSE PARK, `§5.1` clause 1's offence.
4. **THE PROBE READ IS THE SAME DISCRIMINATED READ `§5.1`/`F-7` REQUIRE** (an `isError` reply is NAMED, never a truthiness test) — the per-fixture split changes WHAT is read, never HOW a failure is classified.
5. **WHAT THE ARTIFACT MUST CARRY: the per-fixture verdicts are printed with the fixture state (`§6.1` site (1)/(2)), so a reading can be attributed to ITS fixture** — the run-wide state says WHICH fixture the run used (`G-5`), and the per-fixture probes say what was actually present. **The printed line's clause is `§6.5`'s.**

### 6.5 ⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`⟩ THE CONSEQUENCE LINE: DERIVED, CONDITIONAL, AND SCOPED TO THE GATED POPULATION vs THE EXCLUDED ENGINE FAMILY

**THE CLAUSE: THE PRINTED CONSEQUENCE IS **DERIVED FROM THE DECLARATION'S OWN ENTRIES AND CONDITIONAL ON THE RUN** — it prints only when its antecedent holds for THAT run (a fixture state of `none` AND gated declared keys in the run's block selection), and its figures come from the declaration's entries (the gated count `34`, `§16.3`), never from a constant string. It states what the run DID, not what a `none` state implies in general.**

**THE SCOPE THE PRINTED CLAUSE MUST CARRY, IN TWO PARTS — the clause names BOTH, or it is `§6.2` `X-6`'s offence:**
1. **THE GATED POPULATION IT SPEAKS FOR: the declaration's `corpusRead: true && selfProvisioning: false` entries — `34` keys (`§16.3`), `16` hand-listed + `18` ENTERED (`§16.7`), each parked by name on its declared row id (`21`) or recorded on its `DIAG` line (`13`); and its complements, named as NOT parked: the `10` `selfProvisioning:true` keys and the `3` `corpusRead:false` keys (`§16.6`).** **⟨GATE-7 PROOFREAD PASS `2026-10-05` — THE POPULATION THIS CLAUSE NAMES IS NOW `35` AND ITS ROUTE SPLIT IS `22` `ROW` + `13` `DIAG` (the `UNIT B` cross-unit landing: `47 − 3 − 9 = 35`), SO THIS CLAUSE'S `34`/`21`/`13`/`10` ARE THE PRE-LANDING READING AND ARE KEPT VISIBLE; the derivation, the moved key and the arithmetic proof are at this file's own gate-7 block `§CC-2`, and `§18.2` clause 2's `P/35` insert already carries the population half.**⟩**
2. **THE EXCLUDED ENGINE FAMILY IT MUST NOT CLAIM: the seven `gnosis_*` keys — `gnosis_d2` · `gnosis_wikis` · `gnosis_documents` · `gnosis_query` · `gnosis_status` · `gnosis_doc_update` · `gnosis_crud` — are EXCLUDED FROM THE CENSUS BY NAME (`§2.3` `P-7`: the engine's own documents are not the driver's corpus, `G-2`), carry NO declaration entry, and are NEVER GATED; YET THEY READ CORPUS-KEYED DOM AND EMIT REAL ROW VERDICTS `UF-GNOSIS-1` … `UF-GNOSIS-6` (`gnosis_wikis` → `UF-GNOSIS-1`, `gnosis_documents` → `UF-GNOSIS-2`, `gnosis_query` → `UF-GNOSIS-3`, `gnosis_status` → `UF-GNOSIS-4`, `gnosis_doc_update` → `UF-GNOSIS-5` (a parked row of its own kind, `UF-GNOSIS-5`'s `parkRow`), `gnosis_crud` → `UF-GNOSIS-6`; `gnosis_d2` carries no declared row id — `VERIFIED-BY-READ` of the driver's declared-row table). SO A CLAUSE SAYING "EVERY CORPUS-SHAPED BLOCK WAS PARKED" IS FALSE IN THE SAME ARTIFACT THAT CARRIES THOSE VERDICTS: the clause must name the exclusion — the engine family's precondition is ENGINE-OWNED (`§9` item 4), a DIFFERENT precondition, and its rows are not evidence about the driver's fixture.**

**THE FILED FORM IS SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE AT ITS OWN SITE (`§6.1`'s consequence row): the printed string was a CONSTANT (`UF_FIXTURE_STATE_CONSEQUENCE`) asserting *"a run whose fixture state is none reports every declared corpus-dependent block PARKED BY NAME …, so no corpus-shaped reading in this artifact may be quoted as a live-corpus reading"* on EVERY run — a universal present-tense claim contradicted by the `gnosis_*` verdicts above, and a claim whose words ("corpus-dependent blocks") now name a population the driver prints as `17` while the gate's population is `34` (`§16.9` item 3).**

**FAIL-STATES: (1) a CONSTANT clause printed on every run ⇒ `X-6`; (2) a clause claiming a universal over corpus-shaped blocks ⇒ `X-6` (the `gnosis_*` verdicts refute it in the artifact); (3) a clause printed when its antecedent does NOT hold (no gated key in the run's selection) ⇒ `X-6`'s derived limb; (4) a clause naming no exclusion ⇒ `X-6`.**

---

## 7. THE HONESTY CLAUSE, CARRIED

1. **A FIXTURE IS AN INPUT, NEVER AN ORACLE.** A fixture supplies DATA. It may never turn a proxy probe into a PASS:
   **`D-GP-UFA-2`'s `proxyPASS` rule stands untouched** — a proxy oracle still records `proxyPASS:true` with
   `pass:false`, whether its data came from a real corpus or a mock data set.
2. **EVERY ROW STILL ASSERTS A REAL USER-VISIBLE END STATE ON THE ASSEMBLED SURFACE.** A data set changes **what the
   row is looking at**, never **what the row asserts**. **`D-GP-UFA-3`'s falsifiability + real-input-proof is
   unchanged**: a gesture is still hit-tested (`path === 'cdp'` for `realInput`), a row is still derived from an
   observed value, and no row may return an unconditional `pass`.
3. **A RUN WHOSE FIXTURE WAS MOCKED OR ABSENT MUST BE READABLE AS SUCH FROM ITS OWN ARTIFACT** (`§6`), and
   **a fixture-fed PASS may never be quoted as a live-corpus app reading.** **⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` — THIS CLAUSE IS AMENDED IN SCOPE AND IN FORCE: (a) THE ABSENCE TEST IS PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE (`§6.4`), SO "READABLE AS ABSENT" MEANS *THE BLOCK'S OWN DECLARED FIXTURE* WAS PROBED ABSENT, NOT THAT A RUN-WIDE STORE COUNT WAS ZERO; (b) THE PRINTED STATEMENT OF IT IS THE DERIVED, CONDITIONAL, SCOPED CLAUSE OF `§6.5`, WHICH MUST NAME THE GATED POPULATION AND THE EXCLUDED `gnosis_*` ENGINE FAMILY. THE FILED READING — "a run whose fixture state is `none` reports EVERY DECLARED corpus-dependent block PARKED BY NAME" — IS SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE AT `§6.1`'s consequence row: as a universal printed on every run it is FALSE in the artifact the run itself produces (the seven `gnosis_*` keys are excluded from the census by name, carry no entry, are never gated, and still emit real row verdicts `UF-GNOSIS-1`…`6`).⟩**
4. **THE TWO QUOTING RULES, STATED AS RULES:**
   - **R-`none`**: an artifact whose fixture state is `none` may support **`PARKED`/`NOT-DRIVEN`/`FAIL` readings of
     DRIVER behaviour**, and **never** a claim about a corpus-shaped row's user-visible end state.
   - **R-`mock`** (armed for UNIT B): an artifact whose fixture state names a mock data set may support the rows'
     readings **as readings against THAT data set**, and **never** as readings of a live corpus.
5. **WHAT THIS UNIT DOES NOT CLAIM.** It does not make any row pass; it does not repair any app-layer reading; it does
   not prove the app works. **A green on this unit's arms proves the DECLARATION is honest and the PARKS are named —
   nothing about the app** (`RCA-12`: a node-suite green is envelope-green, not app-green; here even less: a
   SOURCE-derivation green).

---

5. **THE ABSENT-FIXTURE HONESTY READING.** The artifact states the fixture state, the parks name their fixture, and
   **no reading in the artifact is quotable as a live-corpus app reading** (`§7` clause 3). **⟨FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04` — THE READING IS SCOPE-QUALIFIED AND CARRIES TWO NEW LIMBS: (a) the artifact's consequence clause must be the DERIVED, CONDITIONAL, SCOPED clause of `§6.5` — naming the GATED population (`34`, `§16.3`) and the EXCLUDED `gnosis_*` engine family (`UF-GNOSIS-1`…`6` still emit real row verdicts) — and a CONSTANT or UNIVERSAL clause is `§6.2` `X-6`; (b) THE PARKS NAME **THEIR OWN** DECLARED FIXTURE, which is the reading the PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE absence test of `§6.4` makes possible: with the filed single-store-read test, a block declaring `corpus-query-results`/`corpus-document-tabs` can run against an absent fixture, and this reading would be FALSE while appearing to pass.⟩**
6. **⟨added FIFTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`⟩ THE PER-DECLARED-FIXTURE PARK READING.** With the store holding AT LEAST ONE document (so a run-wide non-emptiness test would report "present") and the `corpus-query-results`/`corpus-document-tabs` surfaces ABSENT, **exactly the gated keys whose `fixtureName` is one of those two — `uf_panes_14` · `uf_tabs_7` · `uf_tabs_7_diag` · `user9_search_open_in_tab` — report `PARKED` with `PRECONDITION-FAILED` and a `parkReason` naming THAT fixture, while the `'corpus-documents'`-declaring gated keys carry their OWN verdicts** (`§6.4` clauses 2–3). **The crossed outcome — a key parking on ANOTHER fixture's absence — is a FALSE PARK (`§5.1` clause 1), and a run of a gated key whose own fixture's probe read absent is `§5.5` `F-2`.** **This reading is what distinguishes the per-fixture contract from the filed single-probe form; it is a class-(b) reading (no node row can drive it).**

**A run that is truncated or interrupted proves nothing and must never be reported as a result.**

## 11. THE TYPED PROPERTY REGISTER (code-bearing unit — no exemption is available)

### 11.1 Is this unit code-bearing? YES (stated, so the exemption is not available)

**The deliverable edits `scripts/live-drive.mjs`** (a declaration constant, the runner's gate, the park reason, the
run-wide field). **A code-bearing unit carries a typed register**, per the house rule. **Its subject is the CENSUS +
the DECLARATION's checkability + the park naming + the run-wide fixture state** — and nothing else.

### 11.2 The register (`4` rows — every term printed as the sum of its factors)

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 7/8/12 — THE FILED TABLE, ITS `76` ARITHMETIC, ITS SEED `0x20261001` AND ITS `8×3 = 24` FORM ARE SUPERSEDED AND ARE KEPT VISIBLE BELOW THE CORRECTED TABLE. THE CORRECTED FORM IS A SUM OF TERMS WHOSE PRODUCT SEMANTICS ARE STATED PER TERM, AND THE STRINGS ARE WRITTEN IN THE PIN'S OWN GRAMMAR.⟩**

**11.2.1 THE REGISTER IS A SEPARATE REGISTER — `RECORDED DECISION`.** **THE LANDED REGISTER IS ONE AND IS PINNED: `tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts` declares `const REGISTER_SEED = 0x20260929` WITH AN ASSERTION ON THAT LITERAL, and its `DECLARED_REGISTER` array carries `7` rows (`P-IM-1`, `P-IM-2`, `P-SM-1`, `P-SM-2`, `P-TP-1`, `P-TP-2`, `P-SM-3`) summing to `101`, with `registerArmCensus` scoping its read to the `'§4 <P-ROW>'` describe blocks.** **THIS UNIT'S FOUR ROWS ARE THEREFORE A SEPARATE REGISTER — its own seed, its own `DECLARED_REGISTER`-shaped array, and its own `describe('§4 P-IM-4' …)`-prefixed blocks — because (a) the landed seed's literal assertion would be MOVED if this unit reused it and changed it, (b) the landed `7`-row count is under the ≤ 8 row cap with exactly ONE row of headroom, so absorbing four rows would breach the cap, and (c) `registerArmCensus`'s class census is per-`§4 <P-ROW>`-describe, so a separate register keeps both censuses readable.** **THE LANDED REGISTER IS NOT TOUCHED: its seed, its `7` rows, its `101` and its `85 + 16` split stay exactly as landed.**
**THE SEPARATE REGISTER'S SEED: `0x20261002` (the house hex form; the filing date, per the house convention that the register seed's value IS the filing date).** **The filed `0x20261001` is SUPERSEDED — it is kept visible in the superseded block below.** **⟨The landing pass re-pins the exact literal to the head it lands on; what this spec pins is that the value IS the landing date and that it is NOT `0x20260929` (the landed register's) and NOT `0x20261001` (the filed pre-amendment value, whose date no longer matches the landing).⟩**

**11.2.2 THE STRING GRAMMAR THE EXISTING PARSER ACCEPTS (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `declaredFactorTerms` / `declaredTotalOf` / `declaredStringParses` / `declaredLastTermOf` in the pin).** **A declared string is a SUM OF PRODUCTS with parenthesised GROUPS:**

- **the character set is `[0-9A-Za-z/()+\-×]` plus spaces/tabs; a string outside it PARSES AS EMPTY and is an offence;**
- **a TERM is `k×<class>` or a bare `k`, where `<class>` is the text up to the next TOP-LEVEL `+` (a `+` or a `)` INSIDE a parenthesised name such as `class-(b)` is part of the name);**
- **`declaredTotalOf` = the SUM OF THE TERMS' COUNT VALUES (`k`), a group's value entering the sum ONCE (its inner `+` is added inside the group);**
- **`declaredLastTermOf` = THE LAST TERM OF THE **DEEPEST GROUP** — `VERIFIED-BY-READ` AT THE LANDED PARSER (`reader: this third amendment pass`), which is the rule of record: the parser reads `declaredFactorTerms(declared)`, takes `const top = Math.max(...terms.map((t) => t.group))` and returns `[...terms].reverse().find((t) => t.group === top).k` — i.e. **the `k` of the last term whose group DEPTH is the maximum depth in the string, never the last term at TOP level.** **That `k` MUST equal the row's declared class-(b) NOT-RUN budget, and `declaredTotal − declaredClassB` MUST equal the row's EXECUTED node-side arms (the pin's own accounting: `executed + named class-(b) NOT-RUN === declared`).** **⟨THE FORMER PHRASING, SUPERSEDED AND KEPT VISIBLE (`§15.7` FINDING `F-6`): *"`declaredLastTermOf` = the LAST **TOP-LEVEL** TERM's `k`."* **WHY IT IS SUPERSEDED: it is wrong as a statement about the parser — the parser's tie-break is GROUP DEPTH, not top level; the two rules COINCIDE on every spelling this register currently uses (`(… + 0×class-(b))` ends the string AND is the deepest group, so its values agree), which is why the `0×class-(b)` spelling rule of `§11.2.5` still works and why the error was invisible — but the stated rule and the parser's rule disagreed, and a group-nested spelling (`… + 1×x + (2×y + 1×class-(b)) + 1×z`) would separate them.** **A register string whose deepest group is NOT its final group is therefore OUT OF CONTRACT: the `0×class-(b)` term must be the LAST term of the DEEPEST group.**⟩**
  **⟨A THIRD RULE, STATED SO THE TWO READERS CANNOT BE CONFUSED: `declaredTotalOf` sums ALL terms' `k` (a group's value entering the sum once, its inner `+` added inside the group, `§11.2.4`'s ledger), while `declaredLastTermOf` reads ONE term — the deepest-and-last one. The two are different readers over the same parsed term list and neither is the other's inverse.⟩**
- **every class named in the string MUST be INHABITED by at least one `arm(run, N, '<class>:<name>')` label (the class name is the label's prefix before the FIRST `:`), and the class set must EQUAL the row's own executed-label class set.**

**THE FOUR DECLARED STRINGS, WRITTEN IN THAT GRAMMAR (each row's `declared`, its `declaredTotal`, its `declaredClassB`, and the NAMED arms each term counts):**

| Row | `declared` (parser-compatible) | `declaredTotal` | `declaredClassB` | executed node-side |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`P-IM-4`** | `4×read-procedure-step + 1×non-vacuity + 1×key-existence + 1×helper-closure + 1×frozen-census + 1×self-satisfaction + 1×token-space + (1×phantom-complement + 0×class-(b))` | **`11`** | **`0`** | **`11`** — **no NOT-RUN arm, so the `0×class-(b)` term is carried INSIDE the final parenthesised group, which is what makes the pin's `declaredLastTermOf` read `0` (the group's LAST TERM) instead of `1` (the last top-level term of a group-less spelling). **This is the spelling rule of `11.2.2` applied to the row that has NO class-(b) budget.** |
| **`P-IM-5`** | `3×members-types + 3×uniqueness-resolution + 3×dependency-union + 3×static-evaluability + 3×census-minus-declared + 3×phantom + 3×historical-reconciliation + (3×no-aliasing + 0×class-(b))` | **`24`** | **`0`** | **`24`** — **every class is a SOURCE read and every class's THIRD arm is the NAMED-MUTATION arm; the per-entry reading is an INTERNAL LOOP inside the arms, NOT additional arms (the filed form counted it both ways).** **For the pin's `declaredLastTermOf` identity the last top-level term must equal the class-(b) budget: with no NOT-RUN arms the correct spelling is the parenthesised `(3×no-aliasing + 0×class-(b))`, whose last top-level term is `0`; the simpler `… + 3×no-aliasing` form is ALSO accepted by the parser but makes `declaredLastTermOf` read `3`, so the landing pass must write the `0×class-(b)` spelling — this is stated because the pin's own arithmetic would otherwise break on a legal-looking string.** |
| **`P-SM-4`** | `7×fixture-state-classification + 8×park-naming + (9×row-id-carry + 0×class-(b))` | **`24`** | **`0`** | **`24`** |
| **`P-TP-3`** | `10×run-wide-state-limb + 10×class-(b)` | **`20`** | **`10`** | **`10`** |

**TERM SEMANTICS, STATED SO NO PRODUCT/COUNT IS AMBIGUOUS (`k×<class>` = `k` ARMS, EACH A SEPARATE EXECUTED ATTEMPT):** **`4×read-procedure-step` = four arms — ONE PER STEP OF THE CENSUS READ PROCEDURE (`§2.1.1` predicate · `§2.1.2` read-grammar · `§2.1.3` masking convention + window rule · `§2.1.4` transitive helper closure to the fixed point), and THE ARM SET IS THE FOUR PROCEDURE STEPS, NOT THE SEVEN FIXTURE STATES (`§11.2.3`)** ⟨**SECOND PASS, FINDING 7 — THE FIRST AMENDMENT WROTE THIS TERM AS `4` IN `P-IM-4`'s STRING AND THEN DESCRIBED "one per `§2.1.3`/`§2.1.4` step"; the four steps are now ENUMERATED BY NAME, so the term cannot be read as a subset of the state axis**⟩; **`3×<class>` = three arms of that class (a SHAPE arm, a NAMED-MUTATION arm, and a COVERAGE arm that runs the class over EVERY declaration entry) — NOT `×49`-per-entry and NOT `×3` per entry, because the pin's `arm()` is one executed attempt and this register counts ATTEMPTS, not data points, so a per-entry reading is a LOOP INSIDE one arm; `7×fixture-state-classification` = ONE ARM PER FIXTURE-STATE OF `§11.2.3`, whose SEVEN arms are NAMED AT THAT SUBSUBSECTION (`S-1` … `S-7`) — THE ARM SET IS THE STATE AXIS AND THE STATE AXIS IS THE ARM SET, one-to-one, with NO eighth member; `8×park-naming` = ONE ARM PER FIXTURE-STATE (`8 == 7 + 1`) PLUS the no-id `diagResult` case — **AND THE NO-ID CASE IS A PROPERTY, NOT A STATE (`§11.2.3`)**, which is why the naming axis is `8` against a `7`-state classification axis; `9×row-id-carry` = one per state PLUS the no-id case PLUS the non-precondition THROW case (`9 == 7 + 1 + 1`); `10×run-wide-state-limb` = the ten limbs of `§8.2` `A-8`, ENUMERATED AT `§11.2.6`; `10×class-(b)` = the NAMED-NOT-RUN battery terms of `§8.3`, ENUMERATED AT `§11.2.6`.**

**11.2.3 THE FIXTURE-STATE SPACE, ENUMERATED (`P-SM-4`'s seven states — the state count is named, not implied, because the filed table said `8` while listing `8` items of which one was not a state):** **`S-1` fixture PRESENT · `S-2` fixture ABSENT, block `corpusRead:true`+`selfProvisioning:false` (GATED) · `S-3` fixture ABSENT, block `selfProvisioning:true` · `S-4` fixture ABSENT, block `corpusRead:false` · `S-5` the block CANNOT be driven for a STRUCTURAL reason · `S-6` the block THROWS a RESOLVED precondition · `S-7` the block THROWS a NON-precondition (a defect).** **THE NO-DECLARED-ROW-ID CASE IS NOT A STATE: it is the ORTHOGONAL `row-id-carry` property, which is why `P-SM-4` declares `8×park-naming` and `9×row-id-carry` against `7` classification arms — the filed `8 states × 3 arms` conflated the two axes.**

**⟨AMENDED `2026-10-02`, SECOND PASS, FINDING 7 — THE ARM SET OF `P-SM-4` IS RULED, THE SEVEN ARMS ARE NAMED, AND THE `7`-vs-`8` QUESTION IS ANSWERED: BOTH FIGURES ARE RIGHT ABOUT DIFFERENT AXES (`7` = the STATE/CLASSIFICATION axis = the read-step arm set; `8` = the NAMING axis = the seven states + the orthogonal no-id case), and NEITHER is a count of the other.⟩**

**THE SEVEN ARMS OF `7×fixture-state-classification`, ONE PER STATE, EACH WITH ITS OWN LABEL — the label form is the pin's `arm(run, N, '<class>:<name>')`, class prefix `fixture-state-classification`, so the class term's `7` is INHABITED by exactly these seven labels and the pin's `registerArmCensus` counts them (the `§4 <P-ROW>` scoping is stated at `§11.2.5`):**

| # | The arm's label (`fixture-state-classification:<name>`) | The state it classifies | What the arm READS (so it is node-side and can fail) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | `fixture-state-classification:fixture-present` | `S-1` | the gate predicate's `present === true` branch: the block RUNS and no park is stamped |
| **2** | `fixture-state-classification:fixture-absent-gated` | `S-2` | the gate (declaration entry `corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false`) ⇒ `ufDriverFailureRows` ⇒ `PARKED` + its declared row id |
| **3** | `fixture-state-classification:fixture-absent-self-provisioning` | `S-3` | the gate's `selfProvisioning` limb ⇒ the block's OWN verdict, never a park |
| **4** | `fixture-state-classification:fixture-absent-corpus-free` | `S-4` | the gate's `corpusRead` limb ⇒ the block's OWN verdict (`user6_search_no_flicker`'s shape) |
| **5** | `fixture-state-classification:structural-non-exercisable` | `S-5` | the `SE` class of `§5.2`: a park whose reason names a STRUCTURAL surface, not a missing fixture |
| **6** | `fixture-state-classification:resolved-precondition-throw` | `S-6` | `ufBlockThrowReason` returning a reason ⇒ the `PRECONDITION-FAILED` marker path, never a `FAIL` |
| **7** | `fixture-state-classification:non-precondition-throw` | `S-7` | `ufBlockThrowReason` returning `null` ⇒ the loud `FAIL` AND the declared row recorded by name (`ufThrownBlockRows`) |

**⟨THE `7`-vs-`8` RECONCILIATION, IN ONE SENTENCE, AND IT IS THE SENTENCE A TESTWRITER READS: THE CLASSIFICATION AXIS HAS SEVEN ARMS (`S-1`…`S-7`, above); THE NAMING AXIS HAS EIGHT (`8 == 7 + 1`: the same seven states PLUS the orthogonal no-declared-row-id `diagResult` case, `§11.2.3`'s first paragraph); THE `row-id-carry` AXIS HAS NINE (`9 == 7 + 1 + 1`: the seven states PLUS the no-id case PLUS the non-precondition-throw case, whose declared rows are recorded by name). NONE OF THE THREE FIGURES COUNTS THE READ-PROCEDURE STEPS — those are `P-IM-4`'s `4×read-procedure-step` (`§11.2.2`), a different row and a different axis. A READER WHO SEES `7` TWICE (once as `7×fixture-state-classification` and once as the `S-1`…`S-7` state count) IS SEEING THE SAME AXIS COUNTED THE SAME WAY; the first amendment's `"7×fixture-state-classification = one arm per fixture-state of 11.2.3"` sentence is the reading of record and needs no change — what the first amendment left unstated, and what this amendment supplies, is (a) the seven arm NAMES, (b) the statement that the `4×read-procedure-step` term is the four PROCEDURE steps and not the state axis, and (c) the arithmetic `8 == 7 + 1` / `9 == 7 + 1 + 1`.⟩**

**ARITHMETIC, printed with its terms (the CORRECTED form):** **`11 + 24 + 24 + 20 = 79` attempts total** — **⟨`VERIFIED-BY-READ` of the sum: `11 + 24 = 35`; `35 + 24 = 59`; `59 + 20 = 79` — the terms and the total agree.⟩** — **under the ≤ 400 cap**; **the joint-largest rows are `P-IM-5` and `P-SM-4` at `24`** — **under the ≤ 100 per-row cap**; **`4` rows**, under the **≤ 8** row cap. **THE SUPERSEDED TOTAL IS `76` (`8 + 24 + 24 + 20`), and the delta is NAMED: `P-IM-4` `8`→`11` (the four `§2.1` procedure steps became four counted arms and `P-IM-4` needs no class-(b) term), `P-IM-5` `24`→`24` (the total is unchanged but the form is re-derived: `8` classes × `3` arms, with the per-entry reading moved INSIDE each arm and the `class-(b)` budget written explicitly as `0`), `P-SM-4` `24`→`24` (unchanged, form re-derived: the fixture-state axis `7` and the row-id-carry property `9` are separated, with the naming axis at `8`), `P-TP-3` `20`→`20` (unchanged and now itemised).** **WHAT THE `76` GOT WRONG was not the total but the DECOMPOSITION: `8×3`/`8×3` read as products while the same cells described a per-entry traversal, and `P-IM-4`'s own four procedure steps were counted zero times.**

**11.2.4 `held` / `broken` / `stop-after-5`, DEFINED (`§8.4` item 12 of the review; the pin's own `finish()` is the model and is adopted by name)** **⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 9 — THE LEDGER IS AMENDED IN PLACE ABOVE: FOUR CATEGORIES → TWO (`executed + namedUnrun === declaredTotal`), WITH `skipped` STRUCK AS A CATEGORY. THE `held` CONJUNCTION AND THE TRANSITION ARE RESTATED ON THE PIN'S OWN TERMS. NO FIGURE IN THIS SUBSUBSECTION IS CARRIED: each is a member the pin computes.⟩**:** **`executed` = the row's `arm()` attempts (`registerArmCensus`'s `arm` count, literal + loop-generated); `classB`/`namedUnrun` = its `unrunArm()` calls, each carrying a non-empty reason AND an outcome-class label; `declaredTotal` = the string's `declaredTotalOf`; `declaredClassB` = the string's last top-level term.**
- **`held === true` iff ALL of: `executed + namedUnrun === declaredTotal` EXACTLY; `namedUnrun === declaredClassB`; every `unrunArm` has a non-empty reason AND a labelled outcome class; `counterexamples.length === 0`; and `broken === 0`.**
- **`broken` = the number of FAILED arms (an arm whose check returns a non-null offence string) — the counter the pin's `arm()` increments — so a row with one failing arm reports `broken=1` and `held=false`.**
- **`STOP-AFTER-5`: the first `5` DISTINCT counterexamples are REPORTED, and the REPORTING CAP is reached at the attempt index `stoppedAt`; THE REMAINING ARMS STILL RUN (`§11.2.4`'s amendment).** **⟨SECOND PASS, FINDING 9 — THE FILED FOUR-CATEGORY LEDGER IS REFUTED BY THE LANDED PIN AND IS REPLACED BELOW. THE FILED, SUPERSEDED TEXT IS KEPT VISIBLE HERE EXACTLY AS WRITTEN:⟩** *"The ledger is the sum of FOUR categories and must close: `executed + namedUnrun + skipped === declaredTotal`. `held` requires `skipped === 0` — so a row that stopped early is `held:false` BY CONSTRUCTION and the report must SAY `stoppedAt=<n>`; a `skipped` count that is silently dropped is the accounting offence the pin's `finish()` names (`"N declared arm(s) UNNAMED (a silent drop)"`)."* **WHY IT IS SUPERSEDED (`REFUTED-BY-READ` at the landed pin): `arm()`'s FIRST statement is `run.attempts++` and its return is `executed: true` on BOTH the counterexample branch and the clean branch — THERE IS NO `skipped` COUNTER, NO `arm()` PATH THAT RETURNS EARLY, AND NO `skipped` MEMBER ON `RowState` (`RowState` carries `attempts`, `consecutive`, `stoppedAt`, `counterexamples`, `broken`, `classB`). `stoppedAt` is set INSIDE `arm()` AFTER the arm has run, with the pin's own comment stating it: *"the REPORT cap was reached here; the remaining arms still RUN".* A `stopped` row is therefore NOT an `executed`-short row: its `attempts` reaches `declaredTotal` exactly like any other row's. THE FILED LEDGER COULD NOT CLOSE FOR A STOPPED ROW AND WOULD HAVE READ A LEGITIMATE ROW AS A SILENT DROP.**
- **`§11.2.4`'s LEDGER OF RECORD — TWO CATEGORIES, ADOPTED FROM THE PIN BY NAME (`finish()`):` `declaredTotal = executed + namedUnrun`, EXACTLY.** **`executed` is `run.attempts` (`registerArmCensus`'s `arm` count, literal + loop-generated); `namedUnrun` is `run.classB.length` (the row's `unrunArm()` calls). THE PIN'S `finish()` COMPUTES THE IDENTITY AS `shortfall = spec.declaredTotal − (run.attempts + namedUnrun)` AND REPORTS A NON-ZERO `shortfall` — POSITIVE AS *"N declared arm(s) UNNAMED (a silent drop)"*, NEGATIVE AS *"N arm(s) EXCEED the declared budget"* — WITH `held` REQUIRING `accounted === null`, i.e. `shortfall === 0`.** **`SKIPPED` DOES NOT EXIST AS A LEDGER CATEGORY AND MUST NOT BE PRINTED, COUNTED OR ASSERTED BY THE TESTWRITER: `stoppedAt !== null` is a REPORT-ONLY state, and a stopped row's `held` is `false` BECAUSE `counterexamples.length !== 0` AND `broken !== 0` (the pin's own `held` conjunction), never because arms were skipped.** **⟨THE `held` CONJUNCTION, RE-STATED ON THE PIN'S OWN TERMS: `held === true` iff `run.counterexamples.length === 0` AND `run.broken === 0` AND `declaredTotal − (run.attempts + run.classB.length) === 0` AND every `unrunArm` call carries a non-empty `reason` AND a labelled outcome class. `broken` = the number of FAILED arms (each `arm()` whose check returned a non-null offence string increments `run.broken`), so one failing arm leaves `broken === 1` and `held === false`.⟩**
- **THE `stop-after-5` TRANSITION, RESTATED AS THE PIN'S OWN TRANSITION: on the 5th DISTINCT counterexample `recordCounterexample()`-equivalent pushes it into `counterexamples` (capped at `REGISTER_STOP_AFTER = 5`) and, on the NEXT counterexample only, sets `stoppedAt = <attempt index>` if it is still `null`; EVERY SUBSEQUENT `arm()` STILL RUNS, still increments `attempts`, still increments `broken` on failure — and its counterexample is COUNTED in `broken` and in the `held` verdict but is NOT ADDED to the reported `counterexamples` list (which the report slices to `REGISTER_STOP_AFTER`).** **No arm is DELETED, SKIPPED or relabelled by the stop. The report prints `STOP-AFTER-5 abandoned at attempt <stoppedAt>` — the pin's own `finish()` wording — and that string is a REPORT, not a ledger term.**

**Strategy ids:** `strat:live-fixture-census-derived` (`P-IM-4`) ·
`strat:live-fixture-declaration-agreement` (`P-IM-5`) · `strat:live-fixture-park-naming` (`P-SM-4`) ·
`strat:live-fixture-run-wide-state` (`P-TP-3`). **Every row reports `held`/`broken`; the report prints each row's
declared-vs-executed term.**

**11.2.5 HOW `registerArmCensus` SCOPES THIS UNIT'S ROWS — THE FOUR DESCRIBE TITLES, AND THE PER-CLASS MINIMUMS (`DECLARED_CLASS_MINIMUMS`), BOTH NAMED SO THE LANDING PASS CANNOT BE READ PAST (`SECOND PASS, FINDING 10`).** **`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the landed pin, `reader: this second pass`: `registerArmCensus(src)` opens a row when it matches `/'§4 (P-[A-Z]+-\d+)/` — i.e. the row's own `describe` TITLE must literally carry the string `§4 <P-ROW>` — and then counts every `arm(run, …)` / `unrunArm(run, …)` call in DOCUMENT ORDER until the next such title. AND `declaredFactorClassOffences(row, declared, census, min)` reads its per-class floors from a module-level record `const DECLARED_CLASS_MINIMUMS: Record<string, Record<string, number>>` keyed by ROW ID, whose entry for a row is *the count each declared class term must keep INHABITED*. The landed record has an entry for all SEVEN landed rows and NO entry for a new row, and `DECLARED_CLASS_MINIMUMS[r.row] ?? {}` means a MISSING entry silently degrades to NO FLOORS — so an un-supplied row would pass its class limbs vacuously. THE CONTRACT THEREFORE HAS TWO HALVES, AND BOTH ARE THE TESTWRITER'S TO SUPPLY WHEN IT LANDS THE FOUR ROWS:**

1. **THE FOUR DESCRIBE TITLES (each must carry the literal `§4 <P-ROW>` string, or the row is INVISIBLE to the census and its `arm()` calls are counted under whichever earlier row's title precedes them — a SILENT mis-attribution, not a failure):** **`§4 P-IM-4`** · **`§4 P-IM-5`** · **`§4 P-SM-4`** · **`§4 P-TP-3`.** **These four `describe` titles ARE the census's whole scoping mechanism for this unit's register (`§11.2.1`), and a title typo is the one failure mode that reports no offence at all.**
2. **WHO SUPPLIES THE PER-CLASS MINIMUMS, AND THE VALUES: THE TESTWRITER SUPPLIES THEM, IN THE SAME `DECLARED_CLASS_MINIMUMS`-shaped record (extended with four new keys), BECAUSE THE VALUES ARE THE ROW'S OWN DECLARED CLASS TERMS MADE BINDING — and the spec FIXES the values here so the TestWriter copies rather than invents. `THE RULE THAT MAKES THE VALUE WHAT IT IS (stated because it is what makes a minimum non-arbitrary): for every class a row's `declared` string names with `k×<class>`, the minimum for that class is `k` — the same count as the term — because `declaredFactorClassOffences` ALSO requires the declared term list to EQUAL the row's executed class census (`declaredKeyForComparison` vs `executedClassCensusKey`, both `sort()`ed), so the term count is both the FLOOR and the EXACT figure; the minimum's only job is to make a DELETED or renamed class term an offence even when the equality limb is satisfied by a relabelling. THE `class-(b)` term is NOT in the minimums record: it is validated separately against the row's `unrunArm` count (`census.unrun`).`**

| Row | `DECLARED_CLASS_MINIMUMS[<row>]` — supplied by the TestWriter, these values | Size check |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`P-IM-4`** | `{ 'read-procedure-step': 4, 'non-vacuity': 1, 'key-existence': 1, 'helper-closure': 1, 'frozen-census': 1, 'self-satisfaction': 1, 'token-space': 1, 'phantom-complement': 1 }` | **`8` classes; the sum `4+1+1+1+1+1+1+1 = 11` equals the row's `declaredTotal` and its `declaredClassB = 0` leaves the executed total `11` — the identity `executed === declaredTotal − declaredClassB` holds (`11 = 11 − 0`)** |
| **`P-IM-5`** | `{ 'members-types': 3, 'uniqueness-resolution': 3, 'dependency-union': 3, 'static-evaluability': 3, 'census-minus-declared': 3, 'phantom': 3, 'historical-reconciliation': 3, 'no-aliasing': 3 }` | **`8` classes × `3` arms = `24` = `declaredTotal`; `declaredClassB = 0`** |
| **`P-SM-4`** | `{ 'fixture-state-classification': 7, 'park-naming': 8, 'row-id-carry': 9 }` | **`3` classes; `7 + 8 + 9 = 24` = `declaredTotal`; `declaredClassB = 0`. THE `7`/`8`/`9` FIGURES ARE EXACTLY THE ARM SETS OF `§11.2.3`, so each class term is INHABITED by the arms the state table names — and the naming/row-id-carry arms are the `7 + 1` and `7 + 1 + 1` forms derived there.** |
| **`P-TP-3`** | `{ 'run-wide-state-limb': 10 }` | **`1` executed class × `10` = `declaredTotal − declaredClassB = 20 − 10 = 10`; the `10×class-(b)` term is validated against `unrunArm` count, not against a minimum** |

**⟨ADDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` FINDINGS `F-3`/`F-4`/`F-5` — THE PIN-SIDE READERS' THREE HAZARDS, RECORDED AT THE REGISTER'S OWN SCOPING SUBSECTION BECAUSE EACH ONE SILENTLY MIS-COUNTS A ROW. OWNER OF ALL THREE: THE TESTWRITER. THIS UNIT EDITS NO `tests/**` BYTE (`§12` items `9`–`11`).⟩**

- **`F-3` — THE REGION READER (`scanBalanced`) IS A DEFECT, NOT A CONVENTION.** `VERIFIED-BY-READ` + `RECORDED READING; measurer: the TestWriter's red-set author`: the scanner is entered at an offset with `quote = null`, the driver's prose carries apostrophes inside the bodies it must resolve (`ufBlockThrowReason`'s `` `the block's setup read threw …` ``, `ufEnsureEditFixture`'s own comment) and the scanner has no REGEX-literal handling, so it swallows the closing brace and returns `-1`; the measured casualties are `ufBlockThrowReason` · `ufRunBlock` · `ufDriverFailureRows` · `ufEnsureEditFixture` · `ufSurfaceCensus` · `o0FolderRows`, all reading EMPTY — a PHANTOM offence in every arm built on it — and the pin's own `extractFunctionSource('finish')` is blocked by it too. **THIS REGISTER'S FOUR ROWS MUST BE COUNTED WITH A READER THAT SURVIVES IT — the COLUMN-0 BRACE WINDOW of `§2.1.3` clause 2's amendment (the red set's `ufBodyAtColumnZeroIn`/`ufBalancedRegionIn`), never `scanBalanced`.** **A `describe` block whose own `arm(` calls lie inside a body the reader cannot resolve is INVISIBLE to `registerArmCensus`, so a phantom EMPTY read here would report a SHORTFALL offence against a correctly-authored row.** **THE PRE-EXISTING PIN LINES STAY BYTE-UNTOUCHED BY THIS UNIT.**
- **`F-4` — A LOOP-GENERATED ARM'S SIZE IS READ FROM THE CONSTANT ARRAY ITS `forEach` NAMES INSIDE A FIXED 400-CHARACTER WINDOW BEHIND THE ARM (`RECORDED READING; measurer: the TestWriter's red-set author`; the window is `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `registerArmCensus`'s own `src.slice(Math.max(0, a.at - 400), a.at + a.line.length)` and its `/…\.forEach/` head).** **CONSEQUENCE, AND IT IS THE CONSEQUENCE FOR THE LANDING PASS: two LOOP-GENERATED arm sets inside ONE `describe` read EACH OTHER'S ARRAY — measured, `row-id-carry` was counted as `8` from the preceding `park-naming` array.** **THE RULE THE LANDING PASS FOLLOWS: arms are written as LITERAL `arm(run, N, '…')` CALLS, or at most ONE loop-generated set per `describe`.** **THIS UNIT'S REGISTER ALREADY COMPLIES** (`§11.2.3`'s seven arms, `§11.2.6`'s ten limbs and ten battery terms are WRITTEN OUT; a loop-generated set would also break the `arm(run, i + …)`/`(idx + …)` index form the census reads).
- **`F-5` — `unrunArm`'S OUTCOME-CLASS READER IS FRAGILE (`RECORDED READING; measurer: the TestWriter's red-set author`; the mechanism is `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `registerArmCensus`: arguments are split with a SINGLE-QUOTE regex `/'((?:[^'\\]|\\.)*)'/g` and the class is the LAST literal matching `/^[a-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*$/`).** **AN APOSTROPHE IN A TERM OR A REASON SPLITS THE ARGUMENT LIST AND THE CLASS LITERAL IS LOST, so the arm is counted as `(unlabelled)` — which `§11.2.6`'s own `C-10` rule and the landed `§13.3` arm report as an OFFENCE.** **CONSEQUENCE: BATTERY-TERM AND REASON WORDING MUST AVOID APOSTROPHES (and must keep the outcome class as the LAST single-line kebab literal).** **THIS UNIT'S TEN TERMS COMPLY** (`§11.2.6`: `park-by-name`, `unparked-undeclared`, `run-wide-state-launch-profile`, `run-wide-state-summary`, `early-abort-groups-refusal`, `early-abort-catch-error`, `scoped-block-agreement`, `absent-fixture-honesty`, `search-tab-empty-store`, `no-id-park-route` — no apostrophe in any term, and none in the reasons this spec writes for them).

**⟨ADDED `2026-10-03`, `§15.7` FINDING `F-6` — THE SPELLING RULE OF THIS SUBSECTION IS RESTATED ON THE PARSER'S OWN TERM (`§11.2.2`'s amendment): THE `0×class-(b)` TERM MUST BE THE LAST TERM OF THE **DEEPEST GROUP**, because `declaredLastTermOf` reads the deepest group's last term. `(… + 0×class-(b))` and `… + 0×class-(b)` BOTH end the string with `0` as the deepest group's last term, so both spellings remain legal for a row with no NOT-RUN arms; a spelling that puts any term AFTER the deepest group's `class-(b)` term is out of contract.⟩**

**11.2.6 THE TWO TERMS `§11.2.2` DEFERS TO THIS SITE, ENUMERATED SO `10` IS A COUNT AND NOT A FIGURE (`SECOND PASS, FINDING 7`'s second half).**

**`P-TP-3`'s `10×run-wide-state-limb` — the ten limbs of `§8.2` `A-8`, one arm each, all NODE-SIDE (source reads):** **(1) the module-level constant `UF_FIXTURE_STATE` EXISTS and is declared exactly once; (2) its three members are `state`/`kind`/`id` with the contracted types (`string`/`string`/`string`) and, under Unit A, the contracted values; (3) the `launchProfile` literal carries `fixture: UF_FIXTURE_STATE`; (4) the `summary` literal carries `fixture: UF_FIXTURE_STATE`; (5) the `--groups=` `ARG-REFUSED` print carries the state (site `3`, `§6.1`); (6) the module-level `main().catch` `ERROR` print carries the state (site `4`, `§6.1`); (7) NO site recomputes the value (exactly one occurrence of the object literal in module scope); (8) the statement is NOT inside the `o0Blocks.length` branch (not O-0-scoped); (9) the printed line states the CONSEQUENCE clause (`§6.1`'s consequence row); (10) the `none`-form value is the ruling's literal `no fixture data set selected`.** **The `A-8` mutations (`§4.2`/`§8.2`) each turn ONE of these red, and the arm's failure text must name WHICH limb.**

**`P-TP-3`'s `10×class-(b)` — the NAMED-NOT-RUN battery terms, one `unrunArm(run, <term>, <reason>, <outcome-class>)` each, every one carrying a non-empty reason AND an outcome class (the pin's `C-10` rule):** **(1) the fixture-absent full battery's PARK-BY-NAME reading (`§8.3` item 1) — outcome class `park-by-name`; (2) the unparked-undeclared reading (`§8.3` item 2) — `unparked-undeclared`; (3) the run-wide state on the `LAUNCH PROFILE` line (`§8.3` item 3) — `run-wide-state-launch-profile`; (4) the run-wide state on the summary line (same item) — `run-wide-state-summary`; (5) the `ARG-REFUSED` path's state line (`§6.1`'s print site `3`) — `early-abort-groups-refusal`; (6) the `main().catch` `ERROR` path's state line — `early-abort-catch-error`; (7) the `--block=` agreement reading (`§8.3` item 4) — `scoped-block-agreement`; (8) the absent-fixture honesty reading (`§8.3` item 5) — `absent-fixture-honesty`; **(9) the `OB-3` search-tab exercisability reading in the empty-store boot (`§5.4` clause 3, option (b)) — `search-tab-empty-store`; (10) the no-declared-row-id park route exercised against a real run (`§5.4` `OB-0`) — `no-id-park-route`.** **Every one of the ten names an outcome class, so the declared `10×class-(b)` term is inhabited by ten labelled `unrunArm` calls — which is what `declaredFactorClassOffences`'s class-(b) limb and `registerArmCensus`'s `unrun` count both read (`§11.2.4`).**

**⟨SUPERSEDED, KEPT VISIBLE — THE FILED TABLE AND ITS ARITHMETIC: the four rows as first written carried the members `fixtureDependency` (superseded in `§4.1`), the property texts quoted in this block, the attempt terms `1 non-vacuity arm + 1 key-existence arm + 1 helper-closure arm + 1 procedure-completeness arm + 1 frozen-census arm + 1 self-satisfaction arm + 1 token-space arm + 1 phantom-complement arm = 8` (`P-IM-4`), `8 arm CLASSES × 3 arms = 24` with "each class's arms run PER DECLARATION ENTRY over the DECLARATION'S `49` ENTRIES" (`P-IM-5`), `8 states × 3 arms = 24` (`P-SM-4`), and `2 print-site arms + 1 single-read arm + 1 not-O-0-scoped arm + 1 consequence-clause arm + 1 none-value arm + 1 honesty arm + 1 quoting-rule arm + 2 negative draws + 10×class-(b) = 20` (`P-TP-3`); the arithmetic line `8 + 24 + 24 + 20 = 76 attempts total — under the ≤ 400 cap; the joint-largest rows are P-IM-5 and P-SM-4 at 24 … stop-after-5 on every row (≤ 5 distinct counterexamples reported, then the row stops). Seed 0x20261001 (the house hex form; the minting date is the landing pass's to pin).`** **WHY EACH IS SUPERSEDED: `8×3 = 24` while the same cell said the arms run PER ENTRY over `49` entries (`49 × 3 × 8 ≠ 24`), so `76` held only against an ambiguous definition of the term; `8 states × 3` conflated the fixture-state axis with the row-id-carry property; `8` for `P-IM-4` did not count its own four procedure steps; `P-TP-3`'s ten limbs were not itemised; the seed's date no longer matched the landing; and `held`/`broken`/the `stop-after-5` transition were never defined.**⟩**

### 11.3 The register's typing and honesty limits

1. **TYPING: `P-IM-*` / `P-SM-*` / `P-TP-*` ONLY.** **No `F-` row** appears in this section, and **no `§6`/`FS-n`
   citation appears in the register** (the register's own source text is checked for both, per the pin's existing
   typing rule). **⟨amended `2026-10-02`: the register's ROWS carry no `F-` row id; the `§5.5` `F-n` FORBIDDEN-OUTCOME labels cited from this section's prose are section cross-references, not register rows.⟩**
2. **`P-IM-4`/`P-IM-5` are SOURCE-DERIVATION arms** and are the register's strongest terms in node; **`P-SM-4`'s
   behavioural half and `P-TP-3`'s battery half are class (b)** — **a row that claims a live arm it cannot run in node
   is the self-verification `RCA-1` exists to catch.** **⟨amended `2026-10-02`: `P-SM-4`'s SEVEN classification arms and its row-id-carry limbs are READ FROM THE DRIVER'S OWN SOURCE (node-side); only the state-execution half is class (b), and `P-TP-3`'s ten battery terms are named NOT-RUN.⟩**
3. **`P-TP-3`'s class-(b) budget is `10` of its `20`** — **the largest class-(b) term in this register, and it is
   stated so no reader mistakes the node-side `10` for the battery's readings.**
4. **A row whose arm cannot fail must be re-derived against its DECLARED terms, never re-scoped.**
5. **This register is FILED, not RUN.** The TestWriter lands it with the `§8.2` arms; the counts above are the
   declaration the TestWriter must reproduce or re-state with both values visible.
6. **⟨added `2026-10-02`, GATE-2 FINDINGS 7/8 — THE REGISTER'S OWN `declared` STRINGS ARE PART OF THE CONTRACT, NOT PROSE: the TestWriter writes them INTO its `DECLARED_REGISTER`-shaped array in the `11.2.2` grammar, and the pin's arithmetic (`declaredTotalOf`, `declaredLastTermOf`, `declaredFactorClassOffences`) must hold on them as written. A string that reads correctly to a human but does not parse (`declaredStringParses === false`) is a TESTWRITER-visible defect, and the `0×class-(b)` spelling rule of `11.2.2` is binding for any row with no NOT-RUN arms.⟩**
7. **⟨added `2026-10-02` — THE SEPARATE REGISTER'S OWN SEED CARRIES THE SAME ASSERTION DISCIPLINE: `expect(UF_FIXTURE_REGISTER_SEED).toBe(0x20261002)` (the landing pass re-pins the literal to its own date), and the LANDED `REGISTER_SEED = 0x20260929` assertion STAYS EXACTLY AS IT IS.⟩**

**§16.1 RULE 1 — THE HAND-LIST vs THE CENSUS RELATION IS `H \ C = ∅`.**

**THE RULE (binding for every figure, every arm and every register term of this file): EVERY ONE OF THE `17` KEYS OF THE HISTORICAL HAND-LIST `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS` IS A CENSUS MEMBER — `H \ C = ∅`.** **The landed driver's own printed reconciliation is the reading of record: `historical=17 · ENTERED (census-minus-historical)=30 · LEFT (historical-minus-census)=[]` (`§16.0` item 3); the arithmetic closes exactly — `census = 47 = 17 + 30` — and `census-minus-declared=0` says every one of the `47` is DECLARED.** **THE HAND-LIST'S MEMBERSHIP IS NOT RE-OPENED AND NOT TOUCHED: the `17` keys stay the `17` keys (the literal of `§0.3` `V-1`'s amendment), and what this rule changes is only the RELATION the file states between the two sets.**

**WHAT "HAND-LISTED" MEANS, STATED SO IT STOPS BEING READ AS "CORPUS-DEPENDENT": A HAND-LISTED KEY IS NOT THE SAME THING AS A CORPUS-DEPENDENT ONE.** `UF_CORPUS_DEPENDENT_BLOCKS` is the HISTORICAL set of blocks whose own SETUP read a seeded corpus (`§0.3` `V-6`); it is a SUBSET of `§2.1.1`'s predicate at this head, and it was never a superset, never a complement, and never a classifier of corpus dependence. **`H \ C = ∅` says exactly one thing: NO hand-listed key is corpus-independent. BOTH OF ITS INPUTS ARE WRONG AT THIS HEAD: the hand-list's in-census count is `17` (not `15`), and the three keys it called `selfProvisioning:true` are GATED (not self-provisioning — `§16.4`).**

**§16.4 RULE 4 — THE `selfProvisioning` PREDICATE, STATED, AND THE THREE KEYS `§5.2` NAMED.**

**THE PREDICATE (binding; it is `§4.1`'s member made operational): A DECLARATION ENTRY'S `selfProvisioning` IS `true` IFF THE BLOCK'S OWN BODY **WRITES** A DOCUMENT (or its own edit fixture) **AND IMPORTS** IT (the `edit.import_markdown` store-write call) **AND THEN READS BACK FROM THE STORE THE CONTENT IT PLACED** — the `boot_landing` shape: `writeFileSync` → `edit.import_markdown` → the store document list / the landing surface. THE TEST IS "SUPPLIES ITS OWN INPUT", NEVER "READS A CORPUS".** **A block that reads a PRE-EXISTING corpus document — including one whose read is only an `open`/`focus` of a document whose id it already knows — is `corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false` and IS GATED: its park is an HONEST PARK (`§5.1` clause 6: it is that RUN's legal outcome AND an OPEN obligation on UNIT B, `OB-1`), never a false park.**

**THE THREE KEYS `§5.2` NAMED AS `selfProvisioning:true` ARE NOT SELF-PROVISIONING: `v1_adjacency` · `v2_scoped` · `user4_main_editable`.** Each reads a PRE-EXISTING corpus — the hard-coded `.live-corpus/alpha` / `.live-corpus/beta` documents and the store's own document list — and **none of the three WRITES a document or calls `edit.import_markdown`, so the write+import+read-back triple is ABSENT from all three bodies.** Their landed entries are `corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false` + `fixtureName:'corpus-documents'`, and **the landed driver LEAVES THEM GATED — an HONEST PARK; a class-(b) reading in which either of them carried a self-provisioning verdict would be reading the wrong branch.** **⟨SUPERSEDED, KEPT VISIBLE (`2026-10-04`): `§5.2`'s second amendment read *"THE `3` NOT PARKED (read as `selfProvisioning:true`) ARE: `v1_adjacency` · `v2_scoped` · `user4_main_editable`"*, deriving it from *"`15 − 3 = 12`"*. BOTH STATEMENTS ARE SUPERSEDED: those three are GATED, and the hand-list's `17` keys are ALL `corpusRead:true`.**⟩**

**THE LANDED CLASSIFICATION, BY THE RULE — `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the landed literal, key by key:**
- **`selfProvisioning:true` — `10` KEYS, NAMED:** `boot_landing` · `import1` · `ms_store` · `u_edit_1_live_selection_span` · `u_edit_1_live_caret_head_body` · `u_edit_1_live_typed_commit_one_batch` · `u_edit_1_live_representation_mode` · `u_edit_1_live_head_split_and_textarea_census` · `u_edit_1_live_package_table_limitation` · `u_edit_1_live_caret_roundtrip`. **`§4.1`'s member row already NAMED this same set** ("`boot_landing`, `import1`, `ms_store`, the seven `u_edit_1_live_*` keys other than `u_edit_1_live_commit_failure_warning`" — `3 + 7 = 10`), **so the landed `10` CORROBORATES `§4.1`; what was wrong was `§5.2`'s `3`/`12` reading and every figure derived from it.**
- **`corpusRead:false` — `3` KEYS, NAMED:** `user6_search_no_flicker` · `stage_search_open_in_tab` · `stage_tabs_persist_roundtrip`. **ALL THREE are UNDECLARED by the hand-list (it never carried any of them), so `§2.2`'s old "`1` in the hand-list + `2` outside it" form is superseded: it is `0 + 3`.**
- **THE HAND-LIST'S OWN SPLIT IS `16` GATED + `1` NOT GATED (`u_edit_1_live_package_table_limitation`) — NOT `15` + `3`.** Its `17` keys are ALL `corpusRead:true` (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`), so the hand-list contributes `16` to the gated `34` and `1` to the never-gated `10`... **⟨the `17` all-`corpusRead:true` reading and the `16 + 1` split are stated here; `§4.1`'s own member row's `10` is the same set read from the other side.⟩**
- **CONSEQUENCE FOR THE PER-ROW CLASSES OF `§5.2`: A ROW'S CLASS FOLLOWS ITS DECLARATION ENTRY, NOT THE HAND-LIST — `SELF` iff `selfProvisioning:true` (never gated); GATED / `FM`-subject iff `corpusRead:true` + `selfProvisioning:false`; `corpusRead:false` iff the entry says so (its own verdict from its own body).** **Every row of `§5.2`'s table is UNCHANGED by this rule EXCEPT the two amendments recorded at their own sites: the `#8`/`#9`/`#11` reading (`§16.3` and this section) and row `#35` (`§16.9` item 1).**

**§16.5 RULE 5 — THE `M-null`/AMBIGUITY RULE STANDS AS AMENDED (`§2.1.1`'s clauses `A`–`D`), WITH ITS PRINTED LIST.**

**THE RULE STANDS, UNCHANGED AND RE-AFFIRMED (`§15.7` `F-1`/`F-2`): a census member needs (a) a class-`M` read IN CALL POSITION whose result is consumed as store content OR whose arguments name a document/node id (`2.1.1-A`/`-B`/`-C`), or (b) a class-`S` read that USES a corpus identity (`2.1.1-D`), or (c) a position inside a helper whose OWN body carries (a)/(b), reached transitively to a fixed point with no hop limit (`2.1.4`). A store-method name in PROSE is not a store read (`2.1.1-B`); a bare `rag.query` whose result is never read as documents is `M-null` (`2.1.1-C`, `§2.3` `P-8`); the edit surface read as an OPAQUE presence/agreement signal is not a corpus-identity read and seeds no closure (`2.1.1-D`).** **AN AMBIGUOUS READ IS EXCLUDED AND RECORDED — NEVER A SILENT PASS: `A-1`'s obligation (ii) therefore STANDS IN FULL (the arm must print its own derived key SET, the PER-KEY difference against `§2.2`'s table, and the AMBIGUITY LIST naming each ambiguous key with its SITE and the CLAUSE applied; a recorded ambiguity is a REVIEW FINDING, not an arm failure, and an admission requires a positive non-ambiguous token).** **THE LANDED DRIVER PRINTS THE LIST, AND ITS TWO MEMBERS ARE EXACTLY THE RULING'S TWO KEYS (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the print site and at `UF_FIXTURE_RECONCILIATION.ambiguity`): `stage_boot_landing_diag @ ufSurfacePresence (the edit-surface marker read as an opaque presence/agreement signal) [2.1.1-D + 2.1.4] EXCLUDED and RECORDED` · `stage_multimount_reachability @ ufSurfacePresence (the same opaque presence/agreement read) [2.1.1-D + 2.1.4] EXCLUDED and RECORDED`.** **NEITHER KEY IS A CENSUS MEMBER (`§2.3` `P-6`/`P-11`), NEITHER IS A SILENT PASS, AND A LANDED DERIVATION THAT ADMITS EITHER MUST PRINT IT (`§2.2`'s recorded counter-reading at `2.1.1-D`).** **THE `2.1.1-D` RULING'S COUNTER-READING THEREFORE SURVIVES THIS AMENDMENT UNCHANGED (`§15.9` item (b)); the clause governs, the list records, and the arm prints.**

2. **THE LANDED ARTIFACT INSTEAD DECLARES THE FAMILY AS ITS OWN CONSTANT (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`, `reader: this sixth amendment pass`):** **`UF_EXCLUDED_ENGINE_FAMILY` carries `fixtureName: 'engine-documents'` — the fixture THE FAMILY'S OWN PRECONDITION belongs to — plus its `7` KEY NAMES (`gnosis_d2` · `gnosis_wikis` · `gnosis_documents` · `gnosis_query` · `gnosis_status` · `gnosis_doc_update` · `gnosis_crud`), its `6` ROW VERDICTS (`UF-GNOSIS-1` … `UF-GNOSIS-6`), and `clause: '§2.3 P-7'`.** **THE FAMILY'S FIXTURE IS UNIT B'S TO SUPPLY AND THE DRIVER FAKES NO PROBE FOR IT (a REAL open obligation, never a substituted `rag.list_documents` reading).**
3. **IT IS PRINTED, WITH ITS SCOPE, ON THE `FIXTURE DECLARATION` LINE — and the printed form is the contract:** `EXCLUDED ENGINE FAMILY (declared and scoped, never silently widened)= 7 gnosis_* key(s) […] read the engine-documents fixture and carrying REAL row verdicts […] — OUT of the corpus census by §2.3 P-7, so NO entry in this 47-entry declaration, never gated, NOT covered by the fixture-absent park clause, and their fixture's own probe/read is a SEPARATE open obligation (never a faked probe)`. **THE `GATE SCOPE` CLAUSE NAMES THE EXCLUSION TOO (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `ufFixtureGateObservation`'s `scope`):** *… the `7` `gnosis_*` engine-family keys read a DIFFERENT fixture (`engine-documents`) and are NOT covered by this clause (`§2.3 P-7`)*. **SO THE ENGINE FAMILY APPEARS IN THE ARTIFACT IN THREE PLACES (the declaration line, the gate-scope clause, and its own row verdicts) AND IN NO PARK.**
4. **WHAT THIS REPLACES, STATED AS THE CONTRACT FOR THE FAMILY: DECLARED (in its own constant, with its own fixture NAME) AND SCOPED (named by the clause as excluded) — NEVER GATED (no `gnosis_*` key carries a `UF_FIXTURE_DECLARATION` entry, so the gate's `corpusRead`/`selfProvisioning` limbs never see it) AND NEVER FAKED (no probe is substituted for `engine-documents`, and no `gnosis_*` row verdict is laundered as a corpus reading).** **`§6.5` clause 2 and `§6.2` `X-6` (the universal-clause fail-state) are the clauses that MAKE this shape necessary; `§16.6`'s ambiguity-list row and `§16.9` item 3's relabelling obligation are unaffected.**
5. **A NEW CONTRADICTION FOUND BY THIS PASS, NAMED AND NOT FIXED SILENTLY (see also `§18.9` and `§18.8` item 4):** **`§17.3`/`§6.5`'s exclusion list names the ROW verdicts `UF-GNOSIS-1` … `UF-GNOSIS-6` for SEVEN keys while saying `gnosis_d2` *"carries no declared row id"* — `VERIFIED-BY-READ`: the landed family constant's `keys.length = 7` and its `rows.length = 6`, with `gnosis_d2` the rowless key. THE TWO STATEMENTS AGREE; WHAT WAS STALE WAS THEIR EVIDENCE — a later pass must not read the `6`-of-`7` mismatch as a transcription error.**

**§18.7 THE LANDED `E-3` GATE WRITTEN INTO `§4.1` AND `§5.1` CLAUSE 1 (both corrected in place; this section is the derivation the two clauses now cite).**

1. **THE GATE, VERBATIM IN SUBSTANCE (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at `ufRunBlock`'s fixture-absent branch, `reader: this sixth amendment pass`):** **`pre && pre.present !== true && (the block's DECLARED fixtureName own probe RESOLVED true && that probe's present !== true) && entry.corpusRead === true && entry.selfProvisioning === false`.**
2. **THE TWO PROBE LIMBS, NAMED:** **LIMB (i) `pre` — the RUN-WIDE PRE-BLOCK PRECONDITION READ (`ufBlockPrecondition`: one `rag.list_documents` per block, `present = documents !== null && documents > 0`, `kind` = `fixture-missing` under `--no-seed` else `empty-corpus`), whose `present !== true` is the FIRST conjunct; LIMB (ii) `ownProbe` — THE BLOCK'S OWN DECLARED FIXTURE'S PROBE (`ufFixturePreconditionRead(h, opt, entry.fixtureName)`), whose own TWO limbs are `ownProbe.resolved === true` (the probe RAN and was READABLE; a REGISTRY MISS is `resolved:false` and parks NOBODY, with `kind:null` and the extra `declared-fixture-unprobed`) and `ownProbe.present !== true` (that fixture's own reading is absent).** **BOTH LIMBS ARE CONJUNCTS: a block RUNS when EITHER probe reports present.**
3. **THE THREE PROBE AXES THE REGISTRY DOCUMENTS (`UF_DECLARED_FIXTURE_PROBES[<fixtureName>]`), written at `§4.1`:** **(1) `settles` — the probe's `read` settles THAT FIXTURE'S OWN ABSENCE** (`'corpus-documents'` · `'corpus-query-results'` · `'corpus-document-tabs'` each carry `read: 'rag.list_documents'` at this head, so the three corpus fixtures' absence is settled by the SAME store read while their `unsettled` halves differ) **⇒ `present !== true` ⇒ THE GATE MAY FIRE**; **(2) `unsettled` — what the probe CANNOT decide is NAMED and NEVER INFERRED** (`'corpus-query-results'`: whether a query actually PAINTS a result row for a given term; `'corpus-document-tabs'`: whether a document tab is actually OPEN in the strip at the block's own start) **⇒ a `§5.5` `F-2` subject for UNIT B, never a park ground**; **(3) `read: null` — the fixture has NO probe and the gate CANNOT fire** (`'self-provisioned-document'`, `'none'`) **⇒ never parked and never inferred as absent.**
4. **THE CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS WHY `§18.2` HAD TO RE-STATE THE ACCEPTANCE READING: THE GATE'S POPULATION IS `34` KEYS BUT ITS FIRING IS CONDITIONAL ON TWO LIVE PROBES PER BLOCK, SO A RUN WHOSE STORE IS NON-EMPTY AT A BLOCK'S START GATES NOBODY.** **`§6.4`'s per-declared-fixture clause and its five clauses stand AS THE CONTRACT; the landed registry AGREES WITH `§6.4` ON THE `read` OF EACH OF THE THREE CORPUS FIXTURE NAMES and records its own `unsettled` halves, which is what `§6.4` clause 1 requires of a per-fixture probe.**

**§18.8 WHAT THIS PASS COULD NOT SETTLE (stated, not hidden).**

1. **THE "THREE `settles`/`unsettled` AXES" PHRASING WAS INTERPRETED, AND THE INTERPRETATION IS NAMED RATHER THAN GUESSED SILENTLY:** the registry documents, PER FIXTURE NAME, a `settles` string and an `unsettled` value (`read: null` for the two never-probed names) — **THAT is the two-limb/three-value structure written at `§4.1` and `§5.1` clause 1 (limbs `resolved`/`present`; values `settles` · `unsettled` · `read: null`).** **IF THE SUPERVISOR MEANT A DIFFERENT "THREE AXES" (e.g. `A-5`'s predicate limb, `§8`'s route split and `P-SM-4`'s `9×row-id-carry` term as three settlement axes), THIS PARAGRAPH IS WHERE THAT CORRECTION GOES: this pass holds no shell and could not ask.**
2. **THE CLASS-(b) BATTERY REMAINS OWED** (`§8.3`, `§16.10(c)`): the artifact-quoted park reasons, the per-declared-fixture park reading (`§8.3` item 6), the artifact-quoted ambiguity list, and the early-abort sites are all landing-pass readings. **ONLY `§8.3` ITEM 1'S ACCEPTANCE READING IS NOW SATISFIABLE FROM THE LANDED INSTRUMENT (`§18.2`).**
3. **THE OPTION-(a) FLAG/MODE IS `OWED`, NOT DESIGNED** (`§18.2` clause 3): its name, its semantics and its owner are the supervisor's to set; `§12` item `13` files it.
4. **TWO NEW CONTRADICTIONS THIS PASS FOUND AND DID NOT FIX SILENTLY** (beyond the `tabs` offence of `§16.9` item 9): **(i) `§2.2`'s DERIVED-ROW TABLE STILL CARRIES THE FIRST/SECOND AMENDMENTS' HAND-LIST-ONLY `selfProvisioning` SET** (`v1_adjacency` · `v2_scoped` · `user4_main_editable` in the cell whose superseded value is `3`) **WHILE ITS OWN CORRECTED CELL TEXT NAMES THE `10` `selfProvisioning:true` KEYS** — the cell's SUPERSEDED MARKER is in place (`§16.4`) so no reader is misled, but the REPORTED set NAMED INSIDE the superseded span is still the wrong three; **(ii) `§18.6` clause 5's `6`-of-`7` evidence staleness**. **NAMED HERE, NOT EDITED: both live inside superseded spans whose corrections are `§16.4`/`§16.6`/`§18.6`, and this pass's instruction is to leave everything else alone.**
5. **THIS FILE'S `sha256` REMAINS `OWED`** (`§12` item 4, `§15.8`, `§16.0`, `§17`, here): this pass holds no shell. **THIS FILE'S LINE COUNT AFTER THE SIXTH AMENDMENT IS `1508` (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`: the file-read tool's own line census at the end of this pass; instrument named, `reader: the SpecDoc, this sixth amendment pass`). ⟨SEVENTH AMENDMENT `2026-10-04`: THE `1508` IS THE SIXTH AMENDMENT'S OWN CENSUS AND IS UNMOVED HERE; THE FILE'S COUNT AFTER THE SEVENTH AMENDMENT IS `1590`, STATED AT `§19.6` ITEM 5.⟩**

**§19.4 THE ID-COLLISION DISCIPLINE — THIS FILE CARRIES TWO UNRELATED `F-3`/`F-4`/`F-5` ID FAMILIES, AND EVERY SITE MUST SAY WHICH IT MEANS.**

1. **`PIN-ID` FAMILY (`2026-10-03`, the THIRD amendment's register `§15.7`, with its figures at `§15.8` and its recorded owners at `§12` items `9`–`11`):** **`F-3`** = the `scanBalanced` brace-resolution DEFECT (a quote-agnostic entry state; an apostrophe in the driver's prose returns `-1`; six helpers read EMPTY); **`F-4`** = the `registerArmCensus` 400-character-window HAZARD; **`F-5`** = the `unrunArm` single-quote/last-kebab-literal HAZARD; **`F-6`** = the `declaredLastTermOf` DOCUMENTATION correction. **ITS OWNERS ARE THE TESTWRITER (`§12` items `9`–`11`), AND `§17.6` records them as part of the reviewer-verified CLEAN SET to be left as filed.**
2. **`GATE-4` FAMILY (this round, `2026-10-04`, THIS register `§19.3`):** **`F-1`** the ungraded per-fixture gate; **`F-2`** the per-fixture gate's behavioural inertness + the UNIT B deferral; **`F-3`** the `parked=N/34` route-count line; **`F-4`** the arithmetic `ran` member; **`F-5`** the consequence sentence's universal wording (+ its spec half at `§19.5`); **`F-6`** this register's own absence and `§17`'s count; **`F-7`** the two stale `NON_ROW_DISPOSITIONS` prose entries; **`F-8`** the `--home` recursive-delete HAZARD.

**§19.5 THE SPEC HALF OF `F-5` — THE CONSEQUENCE SENTENCE'S REQUIRED WORDING (`§6.5`, `§6.1`'s consequence row; the driver's printed text is being amended in this same round).**

**THE CONTRACT REQUIREMENT, STATED SO A TESTWRITER CAN DERIVE IT: the printed CONSEQUENCE sentence must state that PARKING HOLDS **IF AND ONLY IF THE BLOCK'S OWN DECLARED FIXTURE PROBE READS ABSENT** — the block's OWN `fixtureName`'s probe, never another fixture's reading and never a run-wide count — and it must NAME THE THROW-PATH EXCEPTION, while KEEPING THE `none`-STATE SCOPE.**
1. **THE BIDIRECTIONAL ("IFF") LIMB IS THE CORRECTION.** The superseded universal was ONE-DIRECTIONAL and total (*"every GATED declared corpus-dependent block PARKED BY NAME"*); the required sentence is the per-block conditional — a gated block parks **iff** its own declared fixture's probe reads absent, and a gated block whose own fixture reads PRESENT does NOT park (it runs and carries its own verdict). **THIS IS EXACTLY `§6.4` clause 2's wording, and `§18.2` clause 2's acceptance reading — SO THE PRINTED TEXT MUST MATCH `§18.2` CLAUSE 2, WHICH ALREADY CARRIES IT (`VERIFIED-BY-READ`: *"every parked key is GATED … every other gated key carries its OWN verdict"*).**
2. **THE THROW-PATH EXCEPTION, NAMED.** **A block whose precondition read THROWS does not reach the park route: it is reported as `PRECONDITION-FAILED` with its reason named, never as a park and never as a silent absence (`§11.2.3`'s `S-6` state, `fixture-state-classification:resolved-precondition-throw`; `§5.5`'s `F-4` forbidden outcome — a park with no named printed record at all — and `§5.1` clause 3 item (vi)'s no-silent-absence rule; `§5.1` clause 4's never-an-app-FAIL rule).** **THE SENTENCE MUST SAY SO, because "parks iff its own probe reads absent" is, read alone, silent about the case where the probe itself fails to resolve** — and the gate's OWN `resolved` limb (`§18.7`: `resolved:false` parks NOBODY) makes that silence a real ambiguity for a reader of the artifact.
3. **THE `none`-STATE SCOPE IS KEPT.** The sentence is printed for the `none` fixture state (`§6.3`); the driver's own print site already branches on `UF_FIXTURE_STATE.kind === 'none'` (`VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the print site), and that branch is NOT relaxed by this amendment — what changes is the sentence's own wording, not when it prints.
4. **FAIL-STATE: a printed sentence that is a bare universal, that omits the "iff its OWN declared fixture" limb, that omits the throw-path exception, or that is printed outside the `none`-state scope is `§6.2` `X-6`'s offence.** **THE SPEC'S OWN TEXT IS UNCHANGED BY THIS ITEM BEYOND THE REGISTRATION: `§6.5`'s clause and `§6.4` clause 2 are the contract; what `F-5` adds is the printed sentence's conformance to them, plus this record that the divergence existed.**

**§19.6 WHAT THIS PASS COULD NOT SETTLE (stated, not hidden).**

1. **THE GATE-4 RE-CONFIRM'S OWN REVIEW FILE — `NOT RECORDED`.** **The eight findings `F-1`…`F-8` are recorded here AS RELAYED IN SUBSTANCE, with each one's evidence `VERIFIED-BY-READ` at the site this pass read where the tree could be read (`UF_DECLARED_FIXTURE_PROBES`, `ufFixturePreconditionRead`, `ufRunBlock`'s gate, `UF_FIXTURE_STATE_CONSEQUENCE`, `ufFixtureGateObservation`, the two `NON_ROW_DISPOSITIONS` entries, the `opt.home`/`rmSync` pair), and as `RECORDED` where only the round's own passes hold the reading (the verdict `PASS-WITH-FINDINGS`, the finding ids, the owners). **WHAT SETTLES IT: the re-confirm pass's own artifact, if the supervisor holds it.** **THIS PASS HOLDS NO SHELL AND COULD NOT ASK.**
2. **THE FIRST PASS'S `E-1`…`E-9` IDS ARE THE ROUND'S RELAYED SET, AND THEIR INTERNAL MAPPING TO THIS FILE'S `§17` SECTIONS IS `VERIFIED-BY-READ` ONLY IN THE DIRECTION `§17` → `§19.2`** (this pass read `§17`'s own subsections and items and named the nine); **an `E-n`'s own verbatim one-line statement from the gate-4 artifact is `NOT RECORDED`.**
3. **THE DRIVER'S POST-ROUND HEAD IS STILL NOT THIS PASS'S TO STATE** (`§17.7(a)`): the driver is being edited in this same round for `F-1`'s counterpart (`E-3`'s gate), `F-3` (the route count), `F-4` (the `ran` member), `F-5` (the sentence), `F-7` (the two prose entries) and `F-8` (the `--home` guard), so its `md5`/line count and its new printed texts belong to the landing pass. **NOTHING IN `§19` IS A READING OF ANY POST-ROUND DRIVER.**

5. **⟨NEW CONTRADICTION FOUND BY THIS PASS, NAMED AND NOT FIXED SILENTLY (`§16.9`/`§18.9`'s discipline)⟩ `§8.3` ITEM 1's ACCEPTANCE READING SPECIFIES THE VERY IDENTITY `§19.3` `F-4` FILES AS A DEFECT.** `VERIFIED-BY-READ`: `§8.3` item 1's re-stated reading (as adopted by `§18.2` clause 2) says the reading is satisfied iff the artifact's `OBSERVED SPLIT` reports `parked = P/34` **and `ran = 34 − P`** — **an arithmetic identity asserted as part of the READING** — while `§19.3` `F-4` records that the member's being arithmetic (`declared - parked`) is the very thing that makes it unable to witness that those blocks ran, and that it must be RELABELLED and/or PRINTED BESIDE `blocksRun`. **SO THE SPEC AS IT STANDS MANDATES (`§8.3` item 1, `§18.2` clause 2) THE SHAPE `F-4` REJECTS.** **WHAT SETTLES IT: the driver pass's `F-4` edit — once the member is relabelled or accompanied by `blocksRun`, `§8.3` item 1 and `§18.2` clause 2 must be re-stated to the new form IN PLACE, and this pass does NOT pre-empt that wording (`§19.6` item 3).**
